        Idea:
# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename → "Action Pane")

## Goal

Eliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to "Action Pane" since it's now broader than tasks.

## Concrete requirements

### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)

Currently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add — **below the lock** — a single split button (two halves side-by-side):

- **Left half:** "open message" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.
- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.

When the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).

### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split

- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).
- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.
- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.

### 3. Tighten the task list header

Currently arranged as: "processing" on the top row, then "succeeded" + "failed" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.

### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box

The popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.

### 5. Rename consideration

The pane today is called the "task pane" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to "action pane" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.

## Out of scope

- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself — just relocate it.
- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.
- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.
- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.

## Notes for the planner

- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.
- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.
- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.
- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.
- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing — don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.
- Be careful about scope — the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.

279	              onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(handleOpen)}
280	              onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
281	              className={baseClass}
282	              aria-label={paneTooltip || "Open pane"}
283	            >
284	              {getIcon()}
285	            </Button>
286	          </TooltipButton>
287	        );
288	
289	      default:
290	        return null;
291	    }
292	  };
293	
294	  // ==========================================================================
295	  // RENDER
296	  // ==========================================================================
297	
298	  // Hide on mobile when selection is active
299	  if (!useDesktopBehavior && selectionActive) return null;
300	
301	  const buttons = getButtonsToShow();
302	
303	  // Nothing to show
304	  if (buttons.length === 0) return null;
305	
306	  // Container styling
307	  // TasksPane (right side) should appear above MediaLightbox on iPad/desktop
308	  // but behind the lightbox on mobile phones for cleaner full-screen lightbox experience
309	  const isTasksPane = side === 'right';
310	  const isMobilePhone = isMobile && !isTablet;
311	  const tasksPaneZIndex = isMobilePhone
312	    ? UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_TAB_BEHIND_LIGHTBOX
313	    : UI_Z_LAYERS.TASKS_PANE_TAB_ABOVE_LIGHTBOX;
314	  const normalLockedZIndex = PANE_CONFIG.zIndex.CONTROL_LOCKED;
315	  const normalUnlockedZIndex = PANE_CONFIG.zIndex.CONTROL_UNLOCKED;
316	
317	  const zIndexClass = isTasksPane
318	    ? ''
319	    : (isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked) ? normalLockedZIndex : normalUnlockedZIndex);
320	
321	  const bgOpacity = isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked) ? 'bg-zinc-800/90' : 'bg-zinc-800/80';
322	  const transition = isLocked || (isOpen && !isLocked)
323	    ? PANE_CONFIG.transition.PROPERTIES.TRANSFORM_ONLY
324	    : PANE_CONFIG.transition.PROPERTIES.TRANSFORM_OPACITY;
325	
326	  const positionClasses = (() => {
327	    switch (side) {
328	      case 'left': return 'left-0 flex-col';
329	      case 'right': return 'right-0 flex-col';
330	      case 'bottom': return 'left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bottom-0 flex-row';
331	      case 'top': return 'left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 top-0 flex-row';
332	      default: return '';
333	    }
334	  })();
335	
336	  // On mobile, left/right panes only show one button - make them taller for easier tapping
337	  const isMobileSidePane = !useDesktopBehavior && !isBottom && buttons.length === 1;
338	
339	  // Add safe area margin for iOS home indicator on touch devices
340	  // Only when the pane is NOT visible - when visible, the control sits on the pane, not at screen edge
341	  const needsSafeAreaMargin = isMobile && isBottom && !isVisible;
342	  const mobileStyle = needsSafeAreaMargin ? {
343	    ...dynamicStyle,
344	    marginBottom: safeAreaCalc.marginBottom(0.5),
345	  } : dynamicStyle;
346	
347	  const content = (
348	    <div
349	      data-pane-control
350	      data-tour={dataTour}
351	      style={{
352	        ...mobileStyle,
353	        ...(isTasksPane ? { zIndex: tasksPaneZIndex } : {}),
354	      }}
355	      className={cn(
356	        `fixed ${zIndexClass} flex items-center p-1 backdrop-blur-sm border border-zinc-700 rounded-md gap-1 duration-${PANE_CONFIG.timing.ANIMATION_DURATION} ${PANE_CONFIG.transition.EASING}`,
357	        bgOpacity,
358	        transition,
359	        positionClasses,
360	        'opacity-100 touch-none',
361	        // Taller touch target for mobile side panes with single button
362	        isMobileSidePane && 'min-h-[72px] justify-center'
363	      )}
364	      onMouseEnter={useDesktopBehavior && isOpen && !isLocked ? handlePaneEnter : undefined}
365	      onMouseLeave={useDesktopBehavior && isOpen && !isLocked ? handlePaneLeave : undefined}
366	      {...containerEventHandlers}
367	    >
368	      {buttons.map(renderButton)}
369	    </div>
370	  );
371	
372	  return content;
373	};
374	
375	export { PaneControlTab };
376

51	    const existing = clipsByUrl.get(clip.url);
52	    if (existing) {
53	      const preferIncoming = !existing.generationId && Boolean(clip.generationId);
54	      const preferred = preferIncoming ? clip : existing;
55	      const secondary = preferIncoming ? existing : clip;
56	
57	      clipsByUrl.set(clip.url, {
58	        ...preferred,
59	        generationId: preferred.generationId ?? secondary.generationId,
60	        variantId: preferred.variantId ?? secondary.variantId,
61	        isTimelineBacked: preferred.isTimelineBacked || secondary.isTimelineBacked,
62	        shotId: preferred.shotId ?? secondary.shotId,
63	        shotName: preferred.shotName ?? secondary.shotName,
64	        shotSelectionClipCount: preferred.shotSelectionClipCount ?? secondary.shotSelectionClipCount,
65	        trackId: preferred.trackId ?? secondary.trackId,
66	        at: preferred.at ?? secondary.at,
67	        duration: preferred.duration ?? secondary.duration,
68	        [REDACTED] || secondary.assetKey,
69	      });
70	      continue;
71	    }
72	
73	    // Prefer gallery entries when the same URL exists in both panes because they retain
74	    // generationId metadata that timeline clips for that asset may not carry.
75	    clipsByUrl.set(clip.url, clip);
76	  }
77	
78	  return Array.from(clipsByUrl.values());
79	}
80	
81	function buildRenderedTurns(turns: AgentTurn[]): RenderedTurn[] {
82	  const items: RenderedTurn[] = [];
83	  let pendingToolPairs: ToolCallPair[] = [];
84	  let toolGroupStartIndex = 0;
85	
86	  const flushToolGroup = () => {
87	    if (pendingToolPairs.length === 0) return;
88	    items.push({
89	      kind: 'tool_group',
90	      key: `tool-group:${toolGroupStartIndex}`,
91	      pairs: pendingToolPairs,
92	    });
93	    pendingToolPairs = [];
94	  };
95	
96	  for (let index = 0; index < turns.length; index += 1) {
97	    const turn = turns[index];
98	
99	    if (turn.role === 'tool_result') {
100	      continue;
101	    }
102	
103	    if (turn.role === 'tool_call') {
104	      const nextTurn = turns[index + 1];
105	      const pairedResult = nextTurn?.role === 'tool_result' ? nextTurn : null;
106	
107	      if (pendingToolPairs.length === 0) {
108	        toolGroupStartIndex = index;
109	      }
110	      pendingToolPairs.push({ call: turn, result: pairedResult });
111	      if (pairedResult) index += 1;
112	      continue;
113	    }
114	
115	    flushToolGroup();
116	
117	    // Skip assistant messages that duplicate a preceding message_user result
118	    if (turn.role === 'assistant' && items.length > 0) {
119	      const prev = items[items.length - 1];
120	      if (prev.kind === 'message' && prev.turn.content === turn.content) {
121	        continue;
122	      }
123	    }
124	
125	    items.push({
126	      kind: 'message',
127	      key: `${turn.timestamp}:${turn.role}:${index}`,
128	      turn,
129	    });
130	  }
131	
132	  flushToolGroup();
133	  return items;
134	}
135	
136	function createMessageId() {
137	  return globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID?.() ?? `${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
138	}
139	
140	function getTurnTimestampMs(turn: AgentTurn) {
141	  const timestampMs = Date.parse(turn.timestamp);
142	  return Number.isNaN(timestampMs) ? 0 : timestampMs;
143	}
144	
145	export function AgentChat() {
146	  useRenderDiagnostic('AgentChat');
147	  const location = useLocation();
148	  const isToolPage = location.pathname.startsWith('/tools') || location.pathname === '/shots' || location.pathname === '/art';
149	
150	  const {
151	    timelineId,
152	    timelineClips,
153	    replaceSelectedTimelineClips,
154	  } = useAgentChatBridge();
155	  const sessions = useAgentSessions(timelineId);
156	  const createSession = useCreateSession(timelineId);
157	  const {
158	    isTasksPaneLocked,
159	    tasksPaneWidth,
160	    isGenerationsPaneLocked,
161	    isGenerationsPaneOpen,
162	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
163	  } = usePanesStore((state) => ({
164	    isTasksPaneLocked: state.isTasksPaneLocked,
165	    tasksPaneWidth: state.tasksPaneWidth,
166	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
167	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
168	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: state.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
169	  }), shallow);
170	  const [activeSessionId, setActiveSessionId] = useState<string | null>(null);
171	  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
172	  const [draft, setDraft] = useState('');
173	  const [queue, setQueue] = useState<QueuedMessage[]>([]);
174	  const [pausedQueueHeadId, setPausedQueueHeadId] = useState<string | null>(null);
175	  const [optimisticMessage, setOptimisticMessage] = useState<OptimisticMessage | null>(null);
176	  const [attachmentLightboxMedia, setAttachmentLightboxMedia] = useState<GenerationRow | null>(null);
177	  const [lastSeenAssistantTurnCount, setLastSeenAssistantTurnCount] = useState(0);
178	  const hasAutoCreatedSessionRef = useRef(false);
179	  const lightboxRequestIdRef = useRef(0);
180	  const bottomAnchorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
181	  const scrollContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
182	  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
183	  const hasTimeline = timelineId !== null;
184	  const positionStyle = useMemo<CSSProperties>(() => ({
185	    right: isTasksPaneLocked ? tasksPaneWidth + 20 : 20,
186	    bottom: (isGenerationsPaneLocked || isGenerationsPaneOpen) ? effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight + 20 : 20,
187	    transition: 'right 300ms cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1), bottom 300ms cubic-bezier(0.25, 0.1, 0.25, 1)',
188	  }), [isTasksPaneLocked, tasksPaneWidth, isGenerationsPaneLocked, isGenerationsPaneOpen, effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight]);
189	
190	  const activeSession = useAgentSession(activeSessionId);
191	  const sendMessage = useSendMessage(activeSessionId, timelineId);
192	  const cancelSession = useCancelSession(activeSessionId);
193	  const sessionOptions = useMemo(() => sessions.data ?? [], [sessions.data]);
194	  const {
195	    gallerySelectionMap,
196	    selectedGalleryClips,
197	    deselectGalleryItems,
198	    clearGallerySelection,
199	  } = useGallerySelection();
200	  const clips = useMemo(
201	    () => mergeSelectedClips(timelineClips, selectedGalleryClips),
202	    [selectedGalleryClips, timelineClips],
203	  );
204	  const summary = useMemo(() => {
205	    return buildSummary(clips);
206	  }, [clips]);
207	
208	  const voice = useAgentVoice({
209	    onTranscription: (text) => {
210	      void handleSend(text);
211	    },
212	  });
213	
214	  const renderedTurns = useMemo(
215	    () => buildRenderedTurns(activeSession.data?.turns ?? []),
216	    [activeSession.data?.turns],
217	  );
218	  const activeStatus = activeSession.data?.status;
219	  const isCancelled = activeStatus === 'cancelled';
220	  const isProcessing = activeStatus === 'processing' || activeStatus === 'continue';
221	  const showKillSwitch = activeStatus === 'processing' || activeStatus === 'continue';
222	  const showNoTimelineState = !hasTimeline && sessionOptions.length === 0;
223	  const hasQueuedMessages = queue.length > 0;
224	  const inputPlaceholder = showNoTimelineState
225	    ? 'Create a timeline to start chatting...'
226	    : voice.isRecording
227	      ? 'Recording...'
228	      : (isProcessing || sendMessage.isPending || hasQueuedMessages)
229	        ? 'Type to queue next message...'
230	        : 'Type or press Cmd+Shift+R to talk...';
231	
232	  const handleAttachmentPreviewClick = useCallback(async (attachment: AgentChatAttachmentPreviewItem) => {
233	    if (!attachment.generationId) {
234	      return;
235	    }
236	
237	    const requestId = lightboxRequestIdRef.current + 1;
238	    lightboxRequestIdRef.current = requestId;
239	    setAttachmentLightboxMedia(null);
240	
241	    try {
242	      const media = await loadGenerationForLightbox(attachment.generationId);
243	      if (lightboxRequestIdRef.current !== requestId) {
244	        return;
245	      }
246	
247	      setAttachmentLightboxMedia(media);
248	    } catch (error) {
249	      if (lightboxRequestIdRef.current === requestId) {
250	        setAttachmentLightboxMedia(null);
251	      }
252	      console.warn('[AgentChat] Failed to open attachment lightbox', error);
253	    }
254	  }, []);
255	
256	  const handleCloseAttachmentLightbox = useCallback(() => {
257	    lightboxRequestIdRef.current += 1;
258	    setAttachmentLightboxMedia(null);
259	  }, []);
260	
261	  const deselectGalleryMatches = useCallback((matcher: (clip: SelectedMediaClip) => boolean) => {
262	    const idsToRemove = Array.from(gallerySelectionMap.entries())
263	      .filter(([, item]) => {
264	        const matchingGalleryClip = selectedGalleryClips.find((clip) => (
265	          clip.url === item.url
266	          && clip.mediaType === item.mediaType
267	          && clip.generationId === item.generationId
268	        ));
269	
270	        return matchingGalleryClip ? matcher(matchingGalleryClip) : false;
271	      })
272	      .map(([id]) => id);
273	
274	    if (idsToRemove.length > 0) {
275	      deselectGalleryItems(idsToRemove);
276	    }
277	  }, [deselectGalleryItems, gallerySelectionMap, selectedGalleryClips]);
278	
279	  const handleRemoveAttachment = useCallback((attachment: AgentChatAttachmentPreviewItem) => {
280	    replaceSelectedTimelineClips(
281	      timelineClips.filter((clip) => !(
282	        clip.url === attachment.url
283	        && clip.mediaType === attachment.mediaType
284	        && (
285	          (attachment.generationId && clip.generationId === attachment.generationId)
286	          || (!attachment.generationId && clip.clipId === attachment.clipId)
287	          || (!attachment.generationId && clip.url === attachment.url)
288	        )
289	      )),
290	    );
291	
292	    deselectGalleryMatches((clip) => (
293	      clip.url === attachment.url
294	      && clip.mediaType === attachment.mediaType
295	      && (
296	        (attachment.generationId && clip.generationId === attachment.generationId)
297	        || (!attachment.generationId && clip.url === attachment.url)
298	      )
299	    ));
300	  }, [deselectGalleryMatches, replaceSelectedTimelineClips, timelineClips]);
301	
302	  const handleRemoveShot = useCallback((shotId: string) => {
303	    const removedShotClips = timelineClips.filter((clip) => clip.shotId === shotId);
304	    const removedUrls = new Set(removedShotClips.map((clip) => clip.url));
305	    const removedGenerationIds = new Set(
306	      removedShotClips
307	        .map((clip) => clip.generationId)
308	        .filter((generationId): generationId is string => Boolean(generationId)),
309	    );
310	
311	    replaceSelectedTimelineClips(timelineClips.filter((clip) => clip.shotId !== shotId));
312	    deselectGalleryMatches((clip) => (
313	      removedUrls.has(clip.url)
314	      || (clip.generationId ? removedGenerationIds.has(clip.generationId) : false)
315	    ));
316	  }, [deselectGalleryMatches, replaceSelectedTimelineClips, timelineClips]);
317	
318	  const moveQueuedMessageUp = useCallback((index: number) => {
319	    if (index <= 0) {
320	      return;
321	    }
322	
323	    setQueue((prev) => {
324	      if (index >= prev.length) {
325	        return prev;
326	      }
327	
328	      const next = [...prev];
329	      [next[index - 1], next[index]] = [next[index], next[index - 1]];
330	      return next;
331	    });
332	  }, []);
333	
334	  const moveQueuedMessageDown = useCallback((index: number) => {
335	    setQueue((prev) => {
336	      if (index < 0 || index >= prev.length - 1) {
337	        return prev;
338	      }
339	
340	      const next = [...prev];
341	      [next[index], next[index + 1]] = [next[index + 1], next[index]];
342	      return next;
343	    });
344	  }, []);
345	
346	  const removeQueuedMessage = useCallback((id: string) => {
347	    if (pausedQueueHeadId === id) {
348	      setPausedQueueHeadId(null);
349	    }
350	    setQueue((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
351	  }, [pausedQueueHeadId]);
352	
353	  // Auto-select or create session
354	  useEffect(() => {
355	    if (!sessionOptions.length) {
356	      setActiveSessionId(null);
357	      return;
358	    }
359	
360	    setActiveSessionId((current) => {
361	      const currentSession = current
362	        ? sessionOptions.find((session) => session.id === current) ?? null
363	        : null;
364	      if (currentSession && currentSession.status !== 'cancelled') {
365	        return current;
366	      }
367	
368	      const preferredSession = sessionOptions.find((session) => session.status !== 'cancelled');
369	      if (preferredSession) {
370	        return preferredSession.id;
371	      }
372	
373	      if (currentSession) {
374	        return currentSession.id;
375	      }
376	
377	      return sessionOptions[0]?.id ?? null;
378	    });
379	  }, [sessionOptions]);
380	
381	  // Auto-create session when needed (only when chat is open or voice is active)
382	  useEffect(() => {
383	    if (
384	      hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current
385	      || sessions.isLoading
386	      || createSession.isPending
387	      || sessionOptions.length > 0
388	      || !hasTimeline
389	      || (!isOpen && !voice.isRecording && !voice.isProcessing)
390	    ) {
391	      return;
392	    }
393	
394	    hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current = true;
395	    createSession.mutate(undefined, {
396	      onError: () => { hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current = false; },
397	      onSuccess: (session) => { setActiveSessionId(session.id); },
398	    });
399	  }, [createSession, hasTimeline, sessionOptions.length, sessions.isLoading, isOpen, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing]);
400	
401	  // Scroll to bottom helper
402	  const scrollToBottom = useCallback((smooth = true) => {
403	    const container = scrollContainerRef.current;
404	    if (!container) return;
405	    // Use requestAnimationFrame to ensure DOM has updated
406	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
407	      container.scrollTo({
408	        top: container.scrollHeight,
409	        behavior: smooth ? 'smooth' : 'instant',
410	      });
411	    });
412	  }, []);
413	
414	  // Auto-scroll on new turns or processing state change
415	  useEffect(() => {
416	    scrollToBottom();
417	  }, [renderedTurns, isProcessing, optimisticMessage, scrollToBottom]);
418	
419	  // Scroll to bottom when opening the chat
420	  useEffect(() => {
421	    if (isOpen) {
422	      scrollToBottom(false);
423	    }
424	  }, [isOpen, scrollToBottom]);
425	
426	  // Cmd+Shift+R global shortcut — toggle recording without opening chat
427	  useEffect(() => {
428	    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
429	      if ((event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) && event.shiftKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'r') {
430	        event.preventDefault();
431	        if (!hasTimeline) {

433	          return;
434	        }
435	        if (voice.isRecording) {

437	        } else if (!voice.isProcessing) {
438	          voice.startRecording();
439	        }
440	      }
441	    };
442	
443	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
444	    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);

446	
447	  // Focus input when opened

452	  }, [isOpen]);
453	
454	  useEffect(() => {
455	    setQueue([]);
456	    setPausedQueueHeadId(null);
457	    setOptimisticMessage(null);
458	  }, [activeSessionId]);
459	
460	  useEffect(() => {
461	    if (pausedQueueHeadId && !queue.some((item) => item.id === pausedQueueHeadId)) {
462	      setPausedQueueHeadId(null);
463	    }
464	  }, [pausedQueueHeadId, queue]);
465	
466	  // Clear optimistic message only when the matching turn appears in real data
467	  useEffect(() => {
468	    if (!optimisticMessage || !activeSession.data?.turns) return;
469	    if (activeSession.data.turns.length <= optimisticMessage.priorTurnCount) return;
470	
471	    const hasRealTurn = activeSession.data.turns.some((turn, index) => (
472	      index >= optimisticMessage.priorTurnCount
473	      && turn.role === 'user'
474	      && turn.content === optimisticMessage.text
475	      && getTurnTimestampMs(turn) >= optimisticMessage.sentAtMs - 1000

477	
478	    if (hasRealTurn) {
479	      setOptimisticMessage(null);
480	    }
481	  }, [activeSession.data?.turns, optimisticMessage]);
482	
483	  const sendingRef = useRef(false);
484	  const sendNow = useCallback(async (item: QueuedMessage) => {

487	    }
488	
489	    const priorTurnCount = activeSession.data?.turns.length ?? 0;
490	    const sentAtMs = Date.now();
491	
492	    sendingRef.current = true;
493	    setOptimisticMessage({
494	      id: item.id,
495	      text: item.text,
496	      attachments: item.attachments,
497	      sentAtMs,
498	      priorTurnCount,
499	    });
500	
501	    try {
502	      await sendMessage.mutateAsync({
503	        message: item.text,
504	        attachments: item.attachments,
505	      });

507	    } catch (error) {

513	    }
514	  }, [activeSession.data?.turns.length, activeSessionId, clearGallerySelection, sendMessage, timelineId]);
515	
516	  const handleSend = useCallback(async (rawText?: string) => {

518	    if (!text || !activeSessionId || !timelineId) return;
519	
520	    const attachments: AgentTurnAttachment[] = clips.map((clip) => ({
521	      clipId: clip.clipId,
522	      url: clip.url,
523	      mediaType: clip.mediaType,
524	      isTimelineBacked: clip.isTimelineBacked,
525	      generationId: clip.generationId,
526	      variantId: clip.variantId,
527	      shotId: clip.shotId,
528	      shotName: clip.shotName,
529	      shotSelectionClipCount: clip.shotSelectionClipCount,
530	      trackId: clip.trackId,
531	      at: clip.at,
532	      duration: clip.duration,
533	    }));

535	    if (rawText === undefined) setDraft('');

537	      id: createMessageId(),
538	      text,
539	      attachments,
540	    };
541	
542	    if (
543	      sendingRef.current
544	      || isProcessing
545	      || sendMessage.isPending
546	      || optimisticMessage
547	      || queue.length > 0
548	    ) {
549	      setQueue((prev) => [...prev, item]);
550	      return;
551	    }
552	
553	    await sendNow(item);
554	  }, [activeSessionId, clips, draft, isProcessing, optimisticMessage, queue.length, sendMessage.isPending, sendNow, timelineId]);
555	
556	  useEffect(() => {
557	    if (
558	      sendingRef.current
559	      || isProcessing
560	      || sendMessage.isPending
561	      || optimisticMessage
562	      || queue.length === 0
563	      || !activeSessionId
564	      || !timelineId
565	    ) {
566	      return;
567	    }
568	
569	    const next = queue[0];
570	    if (!next || next.id === pausedQueueHeadId) {
571	      return;
572	    }
573	
574	    void (async () => {
575	      try {
576	        await sendNow(next);
577	        setQueue((prev) => (prev[0]?.id === next.id ? prev.slice(1) : prev));
578	      } catch {
579	        // Leave the failed head in place; pausedQueueHeadId will prevent further drains.
580	      }
581	    })();
582	  }, [queue, pausedQueueHeadId, isProcessing, sendMessage.isPending, optimisticMessage, activeSessionId, timelineId, sendNow]);
583	
584	  const handleNewSession = useCallback(async () => {
585	    if (!hasTimeline) {
586	      return;
587	    }
588	    setQueue([]);
589	    setPausedQueueHeadId(null);
590	    setOptimisticMessage(null);
591	    const session = await createSession.mutateAsync();
592	    setActiveSessionId(session.id);
593	    setDraft('');
594	  }, [createSession, hasTimeline]);
595	
596	  const assistantTurnCount = useMemo(
597	    () => (activeSession.data?.turns ?? []).filter((t) => t.role === 'assistant' && t.content.trim().length > 0).length,
598	    [activeSession.data?.turns],
599	  );
600	  useEffect(() => {
601	    if (isOpen) setLastSeenAssistantTurnCount(assistantTurnCount);
602	  }, [isOpen, assistantTurnCount]);
603	  useEffect(() => {
604	    setLastSeenAssistantTurnCount(0);
605	  }, [activeSessionId]);
606	
607	  const isPending = isProcessing || sendMessage.isPending || optimisticMessage !== null || queue.length > 0;
608	  const hasUnseenMessages = !isOpen && assistantTurnCount > lastSeenAssistantTurnCount;
609	  const showPendingIndicator = !isOpen && isPending;
610	  const showUnseenIndicator = hasUnseenMessages && !showPendingIndicator;
611	  if (!isToolPage) {
612	    return null;
613	  }
614	
615	  let content: JSX.Element;
616	
617	  if (!isOpen && (voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing)) {
618	    content = (
619	      <div className="fixed z-50 flex items-center gap-3" style={positionStyle}>
620	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border/80 bg-background/95 px-4 py-2.5 shadow-lg backdrop-blur">
621	          {voice.isRecording ? (
622	            <>
623	              <span className="inline-flex h-2.5 w-2.5 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-red-500" />
624	              <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col">
625	                <span className="text-sm text-foreground">Recording... {voice.remainingSeconds}s</span>
626	                {clips.length > 0 && (
627	                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{summary}</span>
628	                )}
629	              </div>
630	              <Button
631	                type="button"
632	                size="sm"
633	                variant="ghost"
634	                className="h-7 px-2 text-xs"
635	                onClick={() => voice.stopRecording()}
636	              >
637	                Done
638	              </Button>
639	            </>
640	          ) : (
641	            <>
642	              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
643	              <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Transcribing...</span>
644	            </>
645	          )}
646	        </div>
647	      </div>
648	    );
649	  } else if (!isOpen) {
650	    content = (
651	      <div className="fixed z-50 flex items-center gap-2" style={positionStyle}>
652	        <button
653	          type="button"
654	          onClick={(e) => {
655	            e.stopPropagation();
656	            voice.startRecording();
657	          }}
658	          disabled={!hasTimeline || voice.isProcessing}
659	          className={cn(
660	            'flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow-md transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-95',
661	            'bg-muted text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted/80',
662	            'disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50',
663	          )}
664	          title="Voice input (Cmd+Shift+R)"
665	        >
666	          <Mic className="h-4 w-4" />
667	        </button>
668	        <button
669	          type="button"
670	          onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
671	          className={cn(
672	            'group relative flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full shadow-lg transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-95',
673	            'bg-primary text-primary-foreground',
674	          )}
675	          title="Timeline Agent"
676	        >
677	          <MessageSquareText className="h-6 w-6" />

686	              <span className="relative inline-flex h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-sky-500" />
687	            </span>
688	          )}
689	        </button>
690	      </div>
691	    );
692	  } else {
693	    content = (
694	      <div className="fixed z-50 flex h-[min(520px,calc(100vh-3rem))] w-[380px] max-w-[calc(100vw-2.5rem)] flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border/80 bg-background/95 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur" style={positionStyle}>
695	        {/* Header */}

797	                Thinking...
798	              </div>
799	            )}
800	          </div>
801	
802	          <div ref={bottomAnchorRef} />
803	        </div>
804	
805	        {/* Input bar */}

807	          {queue.length > 0 && (
808	            <div className="mb-2 flex flex-col gap-2">

853	                  </Button>
854	                </div>

859	          {clips.length > 0 && (

863	                isUser={false}
864	                className="mt-0"

871	            </div>

874	          {voice.isRecording && (

882	                size="sm"

885	                onClick={() => voice.stopRecording()}
886	              >
887	                Done
888	              </Button>
889	            </div>

892	          {voice.isProcessing && (

901	              Session stopped. Start a new conversation to continue.
902	            </div>
903	          )}
904	
905	          {!isProcessing && !sendMessage.isPending && !isCancelled && (sendMessage.localError || activeStatus === 'error') && (
906	            <div className="mb-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-destructive">
907	              {sendMessage.localError ?? 'Agent error. Try again or start a new conversation.'}
908	            </div>
909	          )}
910	
911	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
912	            <input
913	              ref={inputRef}
914	              type="text"
915	              value={draft}
916	              onChange={(event) => setDraft(event.target.value)}
917	              placeholder={inputPlaceholder}
918	              className="h-10 flex-1 rounded-xl border border-border/70 bg-card px-3 text-sm outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus:border-primary/50"
919	              disabled={!hasTimeline || !activeSessionId || isCancelled || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing}
920	              onKeyDown={(event) => {

923	                  void handleSend();

927	
928	            <div className="relative shrink-0">
929	              <Button
930	                type="button"
931	                size="icon"
932	                variant={voice.isRecording ? 'destructive' : 'outline'}
933	                className="h-10 w-10 rounded-xl"
934	                onClick={() => voice.isRecording ? voice.stopRecording() : voice.startRecording()}
935	                disabled={!hasTimeline || !activeSessionId || isCancelled || voice.isProcessing || sendMessage.isPending}

937	              >
938	                {voice.isRecording ? <Square className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Mic className="h-4 w-4" />}
939	              </Button>
940	              {voice.isRecording && (

942	                  type="button"
943	                  size="icon"
944	                  variant="ghost"

946	                  onClick={() => voice.cancelRecording()}
947	                  title="Cancel recording"
948	                >
949	                  <X className="h-3 w-3" />
950	                </Button>
951	              )}
952	            </div>
953	
954	            <Button
955	              type="button"
956	              size="icon"
957	              className="h-10 w-10 shrink-0 rounded-xl"
958	              onClick={() => void handleSend()}
959	              disabled={!hasTimeline || !draft.trim() || !activeSessionId || isCancelled}
960	              title="Send"
961	            >
962	              <Send className="h-4 w-4" />
963	            </Button>
964	          </div>
965	        </div>
966	
967	        {attachmentLightboxMedia && (
968	          <MediaLightbox
969	            media={attachmentLightboxMedia}
970	            initialVariantId={attachmentLightboxMedia.primary_variant_id ?? undefined}
971	            onClose={handleCloseAttachmentLightbox}
972	            features={{ showDownload: true, showTaskDetails: true }}
973	          />
974	        )}
975	      </div>

978	
979	  if (typeof document === 'undefined') {
980	    return null;
981	  }
982	
983	  return createPortal(content, document.body);

92	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {

99	function getEffectivePaneHeights(params: {
100	  viewportHeight: number;
101	  idealEditorPaneHeight: number;
102	  generationsPaneHeight: number;
103	}) {
104	  const {
105	    viewportHeight,
106	    idealEditorPaneHeight,
107	    generationsPaneHeight,
108	  } = params;
109	
110	  if (idealEditorPaneHeight + generationsPaneHeight <= viewportHeight) {
111	    return {
112	      effectiveEditorPaneHeight: idealEditorPaneHeight,
113	      effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: generationsPaneHeight,
114	    };
115	  }
116	
117	  if (viewportHeight >= generationsPaneHeight + MIN_EDITOR_HEIGHT) {
118	    return {
119	      effectiveEditorPaneHeight: viewportHeight - generationsPaneHeight,
120	      effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: generationsPaneHeight,
121	    };
122	  }
123	
124	  const denom = 2 * (idealEditorPaneHeight + generationsPaneHeight) - viewportHeight;
125	  const effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight = Math.round((viewportHeight * generationsPaneHeight) / denom);
126	
127	  return {
128	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: Math.max(viewportHeight - effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight, 0),
129	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
130	  };
131	}

133	function deriveLayout(params: {

150	  const editorVisible = isEditorPaneLocked || isEditorPaneOpen;
151	  const generationsVisible = isGenerationsPaneLocked || isGenerationsPaneOpen;
152	  const editorPaneHeight = Math.round(viewportHeight * 0.5);
153	
154	  // When both panes are LOCKED, split the viewport 50/50 so they meet in the
155	  // middle instead of leaving a gap when their preferred heights fall short.

176	  });

178	  return {
179	    editorPaneHeight,
180	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
181	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
182	  };

257	function buildBootstrappedState(
258	  currentState: Pick<
259	    PanesStoreState,
260	    | 'generationsPaneHeight'
261	    | 'shotsPaneWidth'
262	    | 'tasksPaneWidth'
263	    | 'activeTaskId'
264	  >,
265	  input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput,
266	) {
267	  const layout = deriveLayout({
268	    viewportHeight: input.viewportHeight,
269	    generationsPaneHeight: currentState.generationsPaneHeight,
270	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: input.locks.gens,
271	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
272	    isEditorPaneLocked: input.locks.editor,
273	    isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
274	  });
275	
276	  return {
277	    availability: { bootstrapped: true } as PanesStoreAvailability,
278	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: input.locks.gens,
279	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
280	    generationsPaneHeight: currentState.generationsPaneHeight,
281	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: layout.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
282	    isEditorPaneLocked: input.locks.editor,
283	    isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
284	    editorPaneHeight: layout.editorPaneHeight,
285	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: layout.effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
286	    isShotsPaneLocked: input.locks.shots,
287	    shotsPaneWidth: currentState.shotsPaneWidth,
288	    isTasksPaneLocked: input.locks.tasks,
289	    tasksPaneWidth: currentState.tasksPaneWidth,
290	    activeTaskId: currentState.activeTaskId,
291	    isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
292	  };
293	}
294	
295	function buildStateWithUpdatedGenerationsHeight(
296	  state: Pick<
297	    PanesStoreState,
298	    | 'availability'
299	    | 'generationsPaneHeight'
300	    | 'shotsPaneWidth'
301	    | 'tasksPaneWidth'
302	    | 'activeTaskId'
303	    | 'isGenerationsPaneLocked'
304	    | 'isGenerationsPaneOpen'
305	    | 'isEditorPaneLocked'
306	    | 'isEditorPaneOpen'
307	    | 'isShotsPaneLocked'
308	    | 'isTasksPaneLocked'
309	    | 'isTasksPaneOpen'
310	  >,
311	  generationsPaneHeight: number,
312	) {
313	  const viewportHeight = activeBootstrapSnapshot?.viewportHeight ?? getViewportHeight();
314	  const layout = deriveLayout({
315	    viewportHeight,
316	    generationsPaneHeight,
317	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
318	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
319	    isEditorPaneLocked: state.isEditorPaneLocked,
320	    isEditorPaneOpen: state.isEditorPaneOpen,
321	  });
322	
323	  return {
324	    availability: state.availability,
325	    isGenerationsPaneLocked: state.isGenerationsPaneLocked,
326	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: state.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
327	    generationsPaneHeight,
328	    effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight: layout.effectiveGenerationsPaneHeight,
329	    isEditorPaneLocked: state.isEditorPaneLocked,
330	    isEditorPaneOpen: state.isEditorPaneOpen,
331	    editorPaneHeight: layout.editorPaneHeight,
332	    effectiveEditorPaneHeight: layout.effectiveEditorPaneHeight,
333	    isShotsPaneLocked: state.isShotsPaneLocked,
334	    shotsPaneWidth: state.shotsPaneWidth,
335	    isTasksPaneLocked: state.isTasksPaneLocked,
336	    tasksPaneWidth: state.tasksPaneWidth,
337	    activeTaskId: state.activeTaskId,
338	    isTasksPaneOpen: state.isTasksPaneOpen,
339	  };
340	}
341	
342	const initialSnapshot = createInitialSnapshot();
343	
344	const panesStore = createStore<PanesStoreState>((set, get) => ({
345	  ...initialSnapshot,
346	  setIsGenerationsPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
347	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsGenerationsPaneLocked(isLocked);
348	  },
349	  setIsGenerationsPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
350	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsGenerationsPaneOpen(isOpen);
351	  },
352	  setGenerationsPaneHeight: (height) => {
353	    set((state) => {
354	      if (state.generationsPaneHeight === height) {
355	        return state;
356	      }
357	
358	      return buildStateWithUpdatedGenerationsHeight(state, height);
359	    });
360	  },
361	  setIsEditorPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
362	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsEditorPaneLocked(isLocked);
363	  },
364	  setIsEditorPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
365	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsEditorPaneOpen(isOpen);
366	  },
367	  setIsShotsPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
368	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsShotsPaneLocked(isLocked);
369	  },
370	  setShotsPaneWidth: (width) => {
371	    set((state) => (
372	      state.shotsPaneWidth === width
373	        ? state
374	        : { shotsPaneWidth: width }
375	    ));
376	  },
377	  setIsTasksPaneLocked: (isLocked) => {
378	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneLocked(isLocked);
379	  },
380	  setTasksPaneWidth: (width) => {
381	    set((state) => (
382	      state.tasksPaneWidth === width
383	        ? state
384	        : { tasksPaneWidth: width }
385	    ));
386	  },
387	  setActiveTaskId: (taskId) => {
388	    set((state) => (
389	      state.activeTaskId === taskId
390	        ? state
391	        : { activeTaskId: taskId }
392	    ));
393	  },
394	  setIsTasksPaneOpen: (isOpen) => {
395	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.setIsTasksPaneOpen(isOpen);
396	  },
397	  resetAllPaneLocks: () => {
398	    activeBootstrapRuntime?.resetAllPaneLocks();
399	  },
400	  bootstrap: (input) => {
401	    if (areBootstrapInputsEqual(activeBootstrapSnapshot, input)) {
402	      console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap short-circuit (inputs equal)', { owner: input.owner.toString() });
403	      return;
404	    }
405	
406	    console.log('[PanesBoot] bootstrap APPLY set()', {
407	      owner: input.owner.toString(),
408	      prevOwner: activeBootstrapOwner?.toString() ?? null,
409	      locks: input.locks,
410	      isGenerationsPaneOpen: input.isGenerationsPaneOpen,
411	      isEditorPaneOpen: input.isEditorPaneOpen,
412	      isTasksPaneOpen: input.isTasksPaneOpen,
413	    });
414	    activeBootstrapOwner = input.owner;
415	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = input;
416	    updateBootstrapRuntime(input);
417	
418	    set((state) => buildBootstrappedState(state, input));
419	  },
420	  clearBootstrap: (owner) => {
421	    if (owner && activeBootstrapOwner && owner !== activeBootstrapOwner) {
422	      return;
423	    }
424	
425	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
426	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
427	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
428	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
429	  },
430	  resetStore: () => {
431	    activeBootstrapOwner = null;
432	    activeBootstrapSnapshot = null;
433	    updateBootstrapRuntime(null);
434	    set(() => createInitialSnapshot());
435	  },
436	}));
437	
438	export function usePanesStoreApi() {
439	  return panesStore;
440	}
441	
442	export function usePanesStore<T>(
443	  selector: (state: PanesStoreState) => T,
444	  equalityFn?: (left: T, right: T) => boolean,
445	): T {
446	  return useStoreWithEqualityFn(panesStore, selector, equalityFn);
447	}
448	
449	export function usePanesStoreAvailability(): PanesStoreAvailability {
450	  return usePanesStore((state) => state.availability, shallow);
451	}
452	
453	export function bootstrapPanesStore(input: PanesStoreBootstrapInput): void {
454	  panesStore.getState().bootstrap(input);
455	}
456	
457	export function clearPanesStoreBootstrap(owner?: symbol): void {
458	  panesStore.getState().clearBootstrap(owner);
459	}
460	
461	export function resetPanesStore(): void {
462	  panesStore.getState().resetStore();
463	}
464	
465	/**
466	 * Singleton panes lifecycle contract:
467	 * - exactly one mounted bootstrap owner should drive runtime-backed pane state
468	 * - repeated bootstraps from that owner are idempotent
469	 * - reads outside bootstrap intentionally return the unlocked default snapshot
470	 */
471	export function useBootstrapPanesStore(): void {
472	  const ownerRef = useRef<symbol | null>(null);
473	  if (ownerRef.current === null) {
474	    ownerRef.current = Symbol('panes-store-bootstrap-owner');
475	  }
476	
477	  const {
478	    locks,
479	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
480	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
481	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
482	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
483	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
484	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
485	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
486	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
487	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
488	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
489	    resetAllPaneLocks,
490	  } = usePaneLockPolicyState();
491	
492	  const bootstrapInput = useMemo<PanesStoreBootstrapInput>(() => ({
493	    owner: ownerRef.current as symbol,
494	    viewportHeight: getViewportHeight(),
495	    locks,
496	    isGenerationsPaneOpen: isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
497	    isEditorPaneOpen: isEditorPaneOpenState,
498	    isTasksPaneOpen: isTasksPaneOpenState,
499	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
500	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
501	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
502	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
503	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
504	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
505	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
506	    resetAllPaneLocks,
507	  }), [
508	    locks,
509	    isGenerationsPaneOpenState,
510	    isEditorPaneOpenState,
511	    isTasksPaneOpenState,
512	    setIsGenerationsPaneLocked,
513	    setIsEditorPaneLocked,
514	    setIsShotsPaneLocked,
515	    setIsTasksPaneLocked,
516	    setIsGenerationsPaneOpen,
517	    setIsEditorPaneOpen,
518	    setIsTasksPaneOpen,
519	    resetAllPaneLocks,
520	  ]);
521	
522	  console.log('[PanesBoot] useBootstrapPanesStore render', {
523	    owner: ownerRef.current?.toString(),
524	    inputId: bootstrapInput && (bootstrapInput as unknown as { __id?: number }).__id,
525	  });
526	
527	  useEffect(() => {
528	    console.log('[PanesBoot] effect fires (bootstrapInput changed identity)');
529	    bootstrapPanesStore(bootstrapInput);
530	  }, [bootstrapInput]);
531	
532	  useEffect(() => {
533	    return () => {
534	      console.log('[PanesBoot] CLEANUP unmount', { owner: ownerRef.current?.toString() });
535	      clearPanesStoreBootstrap(ownerRef.current ?? undefined);
536	    };
537	  }, []);
538	}
539	
540	export function PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
541	  console.log('[PanesBoot] PanesStoreBootstrapBoundary render');
542	  useBootstrapPanesStore();
543	  return children;
544	}
545	
546	export function __resetPanesStoreForTests(): void {
547	  resetPanesStore();
548	}

1	import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
2	import { requireContextValue } from '@/shared/contexts/contextGuard';
3	import { useToolSettings } from '@/shared/hooks/settings/useToolSettings';
4	import { videoEditorSettings } from '@/tools/video-editor/settings/videoEditorDefaults';
5	import type { SelectedMediaClip } from '@/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSelectedMediaClips';
6	
7	export type AgentChatContextValue = {
8	  timelineId: string | null;
9	  timelineClips: SelectedMediaClip[];
10	  replaceSelectedTimelineClips: (clips: SelectedMediaClip[]) => void;
11	};
12	
13	type AgentChatRegistryValue = {
14	  register: (value: AgentChatContextValue) => void;
15	  unregister: () => void;
16	};
17	
18	const AgentChatContext = createContext<AgentChatContextValue | null>(null);
19	const AgentChatRegistryContext = createContext<AgentChatRegistryValue | null>(null);
20	
21	const noopReplace: AgentChatContextValue['replaceSelectedTimelineClips'] = () => {};
22	
23	/**
24	 * Single app-level provider. Holds a default (settings-based) value that can be
25	 * overridden by VideoEditorProvider via register/unregister.
26	 */
27	export function AgentChatProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
28	  const { settings: videoSettings } = useToolSettings(videoEditorSettings.id);
29	  const [override, setOverride] = useState<AgentChatContextValue | null>(null);
30	
31	  const defaultValue = useMemo<AgentChatContextValue>(() => ({
32	    timelineId: videoSettings?.lastTimelineId ?? null,
33	    timelineClips: [],
34	    replaceSelectedTimelineClips: noopReplace,
35	  }), [videoSettings?.lastTimelineId]);
36	
37	  const register = useCallback((value: AgentChatContextValue) => setOverride(value), []);
38	  const unregister = useCallback(() => setOverride(null), []);
39	
40	  const registry = useMemo(() => ({ register, unregister }), [register, unregister]);
41	
42	  return (
43	    <AgentChatRegistryContext.Provider value={registry}>
44	      <AgentChatContext.Provider value={override ?? defaultValue}>
45	        {children}
46	      </AgentChatContext.Provider>
47	    </AgentChatRegistryContext.Provider>
48	  );
49	}
50	
51	/** Consumed by AgentChat to read timeline state. */
52	export function useAgentChatBridge(): AgentChatContextValue {
53	  const context = useContext(AgentChatContext);
54	  return requireContextValue(context, 'useAgentChatBridge', 'AgentChatProvider');
55	}
56	
57	/** Consumed by VideoEditorProvider to push timeline state into the bridge. */
58	export function useAgentChatRegistry(): AgentChatRegistryValue {
59	  const context = useContext(AgentChatRegistryContext);
60	  return requireContextValue(context, 'useAgentChatRegistry', 'AgentChatProvider');
61	}

# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename → "Action Pane")

## Goal

Eliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to "Action Pane" since it's now broader than tasks.

## Concrete requirements

### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)

Currently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add — **below the lock** — a single split button (two halves side-by-side):

- **Left half:** "open message" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.
- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.

When the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).

### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split

- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).
- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.
- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.

### 3. Tighten the task list header

Currently arranged as: "processing" on the top row, then "succeeded" + "failed" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.

### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box

The popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.

### 5. Rename consideration

The pane today is called the "task pane" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to "action pane" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.

## Out of scope

- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself — just relocate it.
- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.
- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.
- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.

## Notes for the planner

- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.
- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.
- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.
- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.
- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing — don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.
- Be careful about scope — the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.

# Implementation Plan: Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Action Pane

## Overview

Fold the floating bottom-right `AgentChat` popup into the existing right-side `TasksPane` so the pane becomes a vertical split: task list on top, agent message thread on the bottom. Tighten the task filter row, add a "open-message + mic" split button below the lock on the pane control tab (visible only when not locked), remove the standalone popup, and rename the pane's display label to "Action".

**Repo shape (key files):**

- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx` — the right-side pane (header line 191, filter buttons lines 219–272, `<PaneControlTab>` lines 146–160).
- `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx` — already supports `thirdButton` + `fourthButton`. `getButtonsToShow()` (168–196) decides which slots render per state; locked-right shows `['third','unlock']`, otherwise `['third','lock']`.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx` — single 985-line component with `isOpen` state, collapsed pill (lines 649–691), recording overlay (617–648), expanded panel (692–976), `createPortal(content, document.body)` (line 983), and `positionStyle` derived from `tasksPaneWidth` / generations height (184–188). Gated on `isToolPage` (`/tools`, `/shots`, `/art`) at line 611. Owns `useAgentVoice` and Cmd+Shift+R shortcut (426–445).
- `src/app/App.tsx:24,72` — mounts `<AgentChat />` as a sibling of `<AppRoutes />`.
- `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx` — already uses a register/unregister pattern (`AgentChatRegistry`) for `VideoEditorProvider` to push timeline state into `AgentChat`. Good template for adding voice/focus handlers.
- `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts` — holds `isTasksPaneLocked`, `tasksPaneWidth`, plus the recent 50/50 editor+generations split logic in `deriveLayout()`. Untouched here.

**Constraints:**

- Preserve the recent z-index fix and 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations are locked.
- Preserve the AgentChat tool-page gate (`/tools`, `/shots`, `/art`) — outside tool pages, bottom half hides; split buttons hide.
- Don't redesign the chat (per brief). Don't rename state fields like `isTasksPaneLocked` / `tasksPaneWidth`. Display-only rename in the UI.
- Required-provider hooks must throw on missing context (CLAUDE.md). The new "actions" surface is genuinely optional (no panel on non-tool routes), so its hook returns `null` and callers must explicitly hide UI rather than silently no-op.
- Don't touch `.claude/`, `.megaplan/`, or `vendor/fake-indexeddb`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit current behavior (read-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. Confirm PaneControlTab slot model (`src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx:55-67` for props, `168-196` for `getButtonsToShow`, `201-292` for `renderButton`).
2. Confirm TasksPane wire-up (`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx:146-160` `<PaneControlTab>` usage, `190-216` header, `219-272` filter row, `348-369` task list).
3. Confirm AgentChat surface to remove (`AgentChat.tsx:184-188` positioning, `611-613` route gate, `617-691` collapsed UI, `692-976` expanded UI, `979-983` portal).
4. Confirm `<AgentChat />` is mounted only at `src/app/App.tsx:72`.

### Step 2: Extend `PaneControlTab` with a split-button slot (`src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add `splitButton?: { left: PaneControlAction; right: PaneControlAction }` to the `actions` prop (line 60-63).
2. Add `'split'` to the `ButtonType` union (line 17).
3. In `getButtonsToShow()` (168-196), append `'split'` to right-side non-bottom desktop branches **only when `!isLocked && splitButton`**. Do not include in the locked array — this is what hides the buttons when the pane is locked open, per the brief.
4. Add `case 'split':` to `renderButton()` rendering two side-by-side `<Button>`s sharing one tab cell:
   ```tsx
   <div key="split" className="flex flex-row gap-0.5">
     <TooltipButton tooltip={splitButton.left.tooltip} ...>
       <Button ... onClick={splitButton.left.onClick} aria-label={splitButton.left.ariaLabel}>
         {splitButton.left.content}
       </Button>
     </TooltipButton>
     <TooltipButton tooltip={splitButton.right.tooltip} ...>
       <Button ... onClick={splitButton.right.onClick} aria-label={splitButton.right.ariaLabel}>
         {splitButton.right.content}
       </Button>
     </TooltipButton>
   </div>
   ```
   Each half can use `h-8 w-8` (or the existing `buttonSize` halved) so the pair occupies roughly the same vertical space as one regular button.

### Step 3: Add an actions registry to the agent-chat bridge (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add a sibling actions context (separate from clip bridge so registering voice state doesn't re-render clip consumers):
   ```ts
   type AgentChatActionsValue = {
     toggleRecording: () => void;
     isRecording: boolean;
     isProcessing: boolean;
     focusComposer: () => void;
   };
   const AgentChatActionsContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(null);
   const AgentChatActionsRegistryContext = createContext<{
     register: (v: AgentChatActionsValue) => void;
     unregister: () => void;
   } | null>(null);
   ```
2. Wrap children of `AgentChatProvider` (line 42) so both the clip bridge and the new actions registry are available.
3. Export hooks:
   - `useAgentChatActions(): AgentChatActionsValue | null` — returns `null` when no panel registers (intentional; document inline why this is optional vs. the rest of the bridge that throws).
   - `useAgentChatActionsRegistry()` — `requireContextValue(...)` for the panel.

### Step 4: Strip floating UI from AgentChat → `AgentChatPanel` (`src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/index.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Rename the exported function to `AgentChatPanel` and re-export from `index.ts`.
2. Remove the floating-window scaffolding:
   - Drop `isOpen` state (line 171), the route gate `if (!isToolPage) return null` (611-613) — the parent now controls mounting.
   - Drop the collapsed-pill `<div className="fixed z-50 ...">` block (649-691) and the closed-state recording overlay (617-648). Inline recording UI inside the panel (874-897) already covers feedback.
   - Drop `positionStyle` and the `usePanesStore` selector that drives it (160-188); the panel sizes from its parent.
   - Replace the outer `fixed z-50 h-[min(520px,calc(100vh-3rem))] w-[380px] ...` with `flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-hidden` so it fills the bottom half of TasksPane.
   - Drop `createPortal(content, document.body)` (979-983); return JSX directly.
3. Keep: all session/queue/optimistic state, attachment lightbox (it can still `createPortal` since it overlays everything), Cmd+Shift+R shortcut (426-445).
4. Register voice + focus on the new actions registry:
   ```tsx
   const actionsRegistry = useAgentChatActionsRegistry();
   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.register({
       toggleRecording: () => {
         if (voice.isRecording) voice.stopRecording();
         else if (!voice.isProcessing) voice.startRecording();
       },
       isRecording: voice.isRecording,
       isProcessing: voice.isProcessing,
       focusComposer: () => inputRef.current?.focus(),
     });
     return () => actionsRegistry.unregister();
   }, [actionsRegistry, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing, voice.startRecording, voice.stopRecording]);
   ```
5. Remove the now-unused `MessageSquareText` collapsed-bubble badge logic (`hasUnseenMessages` / `showPendingIndicator` / `showUnseenIndicator` / `lastSeenAssistantTurnCount` / the assistant-turn-count effect at 596-605) since there is no closed state. Keep the `Thinking…` line (794-799) inside the open panel.
6. The "auto-create session when chat is open" gate (382-399) currently checks `!isOpen`. Since panel is always mounted, replace with: only auto-create when the panel is rendered (i.e. unconditionally inside `AgentChatPanel`) AND `hasTimeline` AND no existing session — same conditions, minus `isOpen`/`voice.isRecording`/`voice.isProcessing`.

### Step 5: Restructure TasksPane (`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Header rename** (line 192): `<h2>Tasks</h2>` → `<h2>Action</h2>`. Update `display.paneTooltip` (line 150) `"View all tasks"` → `"Open Action pane"` and the aria-label on the `thirdButton` (line 154) accordingly. (No state/file rename — display only.)
2. **Tighten the filter row** (lines 219-272): collapse the two-row layout into one `flex gap-1` row of three equal-width buttons:
   ```tsx
   <div className="bg-zinc-800 rounded-lg p-1">
     <div className="flex gap-1">
       {(['Processing','Succeeded','Failed'] as FilterGroup[]).map((filter) => (
         <Button key={filter} ... className="flex-1 text-xs ...">
           <span>{filter}</span>
           <StatusIndicator count={countFor(filter)} type={filter} ... />
         </Button>
       ))}
     </div>
   </div>
   ```
   Pull the existing `cancellableTaskCount` / `displayStatusCounts.recentSuccesses` / `recentFailures` mapping into a small `countFor()` helper. Drop `text-xs` to `text-[11px]` if the trio overflows on the default `tasksPaneWidth`.
3. **Vertical split**: wrap the entire existing top section (header → filter row → `PaginationControls` → warning → `TaskList`) in a `flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col overflow-hidden` container, then add a sibling below:
   ```tsx
   {isToolRoute && (
     <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 border-t border-zinc-800">
       <AgentChatPanel />
     </div>
   )}
   ```
   Add a tiny helper for the route check (can live local to TasksPane):
   ```ts
   const { pathname } = useLocation();
   const isToolRoute = pathname.startsWith('/tools') || pathname === '/shots' || pathname === '/art';
   ```
   This preserves the previous gate without leaking the chat onto non-tool pages.
4. **Wire the split button** on `<PaneControlTab>` (lines 146-160):
   ```tsx
   const actions = useAgentChatActions(); // null if no panel (e.g. non-tool route)
   ...
   actions={{
     thirdButton: { /* unchanged active-task indicator */ },
     splitButton: actions && isToolRoute
       ? {
           left: {
             onClick: () => { openPane(); requestAnimationFrame(() => actions.focusComposer()); },
             ariaLabel: 'Open message composer',
             tooltip: 'Open message',
             content: <MessageSquareText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
           },
           right: {
             onClick: () => actions.toggleRecording(),
             ariaLabel: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start voice recording',
             tooltip: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop' : 'Voice (⌘⇧R)',
             content: actions.isRecording
               ? <Square className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
               : <Mic className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
           },
         }
       : undefined,
   }}
   ```
   Step 2 already enforces "only render when not locked," so the brief's "hide when locked" requirement is satisfied.

### Step 6: Remove the standalone AgentChat mount (`src/app/App.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Delete the `import { AgentChat } from '@/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat'` (line 24) and the `<AgentChat />` JSX (line 72). The panel now lives inside `TasksPane`.
2. Grep for any other `<AgentChat` usage and confirm none remain (the agent-explore pass found only the one mount).

### Step 7: Update tests (`src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.test.tsx`, `src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx`, plus any TasksPane tests)
**Scope:** Small-Medium
1. `AgentChat.test.tsx` — drop tests covering the closed-pill / `setIsOpen` toggle / floating positioning. Add a test that `AgentChatPanel` registers actions on the new context (mock the registry) and that the inline recording UI shows on `voice.isRecording`.
2. `AppProviders.test.tsx` — extend the bridge test to assert the new actions context is wired (default value is `null`, registry is non-null inside the provider tree).
3. Add a TasksPane test asserting:
   - The single-row filter renders Processing/Succeeded/Failed.
   - On a tool route, `AgentChatPanel` renders below the task list.
   - On a non-tool route, the chat panel is absent and the `splitButton` is undefined.
4. Run targeted vitest first:
   ```bash
   npx vitest run src/shared/components/PaneControlTab \
                  src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext \
                  src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane \
                  src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat \
                  src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx
   ```
5. Then `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npx eslint` on the touched files.
6. Finally `npx vitest run` for the full suite.

### Step 8: Manual UI smoke (info-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. `npm run dev`. On `/tools/video-editor`:
   - Lock pane → bottom half shows the chat thread, tasks above; chat scrolls independently; split buttons disappear from the tab handle.
   - Unlock pane → split message + mic buttons appear below the lock; click message opens the pane and focuses the composer; click mic toggles recording; Cmd+Shift+R still toggles recording.
   - Cancel All on tasks still works; pagination unchanged.
2. Switch to `/` (non-tool) → pane shows tasks only; split buttons absent.
3. Lock both Editor and Generations → 50/50 viewport split still applies; tasks pane z-index still correct relative to MediaLightbox on desktop and mobile.

## Execution Order
1. Step 2 (PaneControlTab split slot) — additive, no consumer impact yet.
2. Step 3 (Actions registry context) — additive surface only.
3. Step 4 (AgentChatPanel refactor) — depends on Step 3.
4. Step 5 (TasksPane wiring + filter row + split button) — depends on Steps 2, 3, 4.
5. Step 6 (App.tsx cleanup) — only after Step 5 so there's no unmounted period.
6. Step 7 (tests).
7. Step 8 (manual UI smoke).

## Validation Order
1. Type-check after each step (`npx tsc --noEmit`).
2. Targeted vitest specs for touched files.
3. Full vitest suite.
4. Lint on touched files.
5. Manual UI smoke on `/tools/...` and a non-tool route.

  "recommendation": "ITERATE",
  "rationale": "Two of the eight blocking flags name concrete implementation problems that aren't just \"be more careful during execution\" \u2014 they need explicit plan answers. (1) `correctness-2` is a real re-render churn bug: `useAgentVoice` recreates `startRecording` whenever its `onResult` callback identity changes, so a registration effect that lists `voice.startRecording`/`voice.stopRecording` in its deps will fire every render, causing register/unregister thrash. The plan must commit to a stabilization strategy (register a `ref`-backed action object, or memoize via `useEvent`/`useCallback`) rather than the bare effect shown. (2) `all_locations-2` is a compilation issue: `AgentChatMessage.tsx` imports the `ToolCallPair` type from `./AgentChat`, so renaming the function without preserving the type export surface will break the build. The plan should call out keeping the named type exports (or moving them to a sibling `types.ts`) as part of step 4. The remaining flags are weaker but worth addressing in the same pass: `correctness-1` deserves an explicit \"first-render shows the splitButton missing for one paint\" acknowledgement plus a test that asserts post-registration appearance; `callers` wants a mounted/current-value guard on the `requestAnimationFrame(focusComposer)` path; `all_locations-1` wants a concrete test for the parent/child registration handshake. The scope/naming flags (`FLAG-003`, `issue_hints`, `scope`) are genuine judgment calls, not plan defects \u2014 the plan's display-only rename and tool-route gate are defensible given the brief's \"be careful about scope\" instruction, and these can be settled in the next revision rather than re-litigated. Since this is iteration 1, no flags have been re-opened, no recurring critiques, and the issues are concrete and addressable, ITERATE is the right call rather than ESCALATE or TIEBREAKER. Sending back for a tightened plan with explicit answers to correctness-1/2, all_locations-2, and callers.",

Posture: high flag count for a first pass, but the flags are concrete and addressable in one revision rather than indicating a stuck loop.",

    {
      "id": "route-gate-preservation",
      "decision": "AgentChatPanel and the splitButton remain gated to /tools|/shots|/art routes; non-tool routes show only the task list.",
      "rationale": "Preserves current AgentChat behavior (gate already exists at AgentChat.tsx:611-613). Removing the gate would expand the agent chat surface to routes that have no timeline context to operate on, which is out of scope for a relocation task."
    },

      "id": "actions-context-nullable",
      "decision": "useAgentChatActions() returns null when no panel is mounted; consumers must explicitly hide UI on null. Documented inline as the one optional-context exception per CLAUDE.md.",
      "rationale": "Genuinely optional surface (no panel on non-tool routes). The 'throw on missing provider' rule from CLAUDE.md is for required providers; this one is documented as optional with a specific reason, which the rule allows."
    }

  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 1,
    "idea": "# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename \u2192 \"Action Pane\")\n\n## Goal\n\nEliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to \"Action Pane\" since it's now broader than tasks.\n\n## Concrete requirements\n\n### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)\n\nCurrently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add \u2014 **below the lock** \u2014 a single split button (two halves side-by-side):\n\n- **Left half:** \"open message\" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.\n- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.\n\nWhen the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).\n\n### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split\n\n- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).\n- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.\n- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.\n\n### 3. Tighten the task list header\n\nCurrently arranged as: \"processing\" on the top row, then \"succeeded\" + \"failed\" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.\n\n### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box\n\nThe popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.\n\n### 5. Rename consideration\n\nThe pane today is called the \"task pane\" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to \"action pane\" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself \u2014 just relocate it.\n- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.\n- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.\n- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.\n\n## Notes for the planner\n\n- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.\n- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.\n- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.\n- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.\n- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing \u2014 don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.\n- Be careful about scope \u2014 the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.",

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Revision 2 delivered. Key changes:

- **Step 3 redesigned** around a `handlersRef` + separate `publishState(reactiveState)`. Imperative methods always read the latest registered handler via the ref (kills the stale-closure path the `callers` flag worried about); the registration effect uses a `useMemo([], ...)` handler object so it runs exactly once per mount, no matter how many times `voice.startRecording` changes identity (kills the `correctness-2` re-render churn).
- **Step 4 explicitly preserves** the `ToolCallPair`/`RenderedTurn` type exports — only the function/default export renames; the file path stays `AgentChat.tsx` so `AgentChatMessage.tsx` keeps compiling (`all_locations-2`).
- **Step 5 calls out** the one-paint delay (`correctness-1`) — `useAgentChatActions()` returns `null` for the first render, then non-null after registration; the split button appears on the next paint, and tests assert the appearance via `waitFor`.
- **Step 7 mandates** concrete tests for: registration handshake on tool route, disappearance when locked or off-tool-route, stale-closure resistance of imperative methods, and `PaneControlTab` slot behavior (`all_locations-1`).
- **Naming-scope and route-gate flags** (`FLAG-003`, `issue_hints`, `scope`) are noted as gate-settled and not re-litigated; reasoning is in the Overview's "settled decisions" block.
