        Idea:
# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename → "Action Pane")

## Goal

Eliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to "Action Pane" since it's now broader than tasks.

## Concrete requirements

### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)

Currently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add — **below the lock** — a single split button (two halves side-by-side):

- **Left half:** "open message" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.
- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.

When the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).

### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split

- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).
- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.
- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.

### 3. Tighten the task list header

Currently arranged as: "processing" on the top row, then "succeeded" + "failed" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.

### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box

The popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.

### 5. Rename consideration

The pane today is called the "task pane" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to "action pane" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.

## Out of scope

- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself — just relocate it.
- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.
- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.
- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.

## Notes for the planner

- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.
- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.
- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.
- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.
- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing — don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.
- Be careful about scope — the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.

# Implementation Plan: Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Action Pane (rev 2)

## Overview

Fold the floating bottom-right `AgentChat` popup into the right-side `TasksPane` so the pane becomes a vertical split — task list on top, agent message thread on the bottom. Tighten the task filter row, add a "open-message + mic" split button below the lock on the pane control tab (visible only when not locked), remove the standalone popup, and rename the pane's display label to "Action".

**Settled decisions (from gate; do not re-litigate):**

- **Display-only rename.** Header/tooltip/aria become `Action`. Code-level names (`TasksPane`, `tasksPaneWidth`, `isTasksPaneLocked`, `paneIcon: 'tasks'`, `⌥D` shortcut hint) stay as-is. Brief explicitly says "be careful about scope".
- **Route gate preserved.** `AgentChatPanel` and the split button stay gated to `/tools|/shots|/art`. Outside tool routes the pane is task-only and the split button is absent.
- **`useAgentChatActions()` may return `null`.** Documented inline as the one optional-context exception per CLAUDE.md, with a comment pointing back to this decision.

**Repo shape (key files):**

- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx` — header line 192, `<PaneControlTab>` lines 146–160, filter row 219–272, task list 348–369.
- `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx` — `actions.thirdButton`/`fourthButton` slots; `getButtonsToShow()` 168–196; `renderButton()` 201–292.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx` — owns `isOpen`, `positionStyle` (184–188), collapsed pill (649–691), recording overlay (617–648), expanded panel (692–976), `createPortal` (979–983), `isToolPage` gate (611), `useAgentVoice` (208–212), Cmd+Shift+R shortcut (426–445). **Also exports `ToolCallPair` and `RenderedTurn` types** (lines 24–31), consumed by `AgentChatMessage.tsx`.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChatMessage.tsx` — imports `ToolCallPair` from `./AgentChat`.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/index.ts` — re-exports `AgentChat`.
- `src/app/App.tsx:24,72` — mounts `<AgentChat />`.
- `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx` — existing register/unregister bridge; good template for the new actions registry.
- `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts` — `deriveLayout()` 50/50 viewport split + z-index logic. **Untouched.**
- `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useAgentVoice.ts` + `src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording` — `startRecording` identity changes whenever the `onResult` callback identity changes. Critical to the registration design (see Step 3/4).

**Constraints:**

- Preserve recent z-index fix and Editor+Generations 50/50 viewport split.
- The actions registry must register **once per mount**, not on every AgentChat render (`useVoiceRecording` returns new `startRecording` references when `onResult` identity changes; registering handlers directly in an effect with `voice.startRecording` in the deps would thrash the registry on each AgentChat re-render). Use a stable ref-backed handler object plus separate reactive-state publication.
- Don't touch `.claude/`, `.megaplan/`, or `vendor/fake-indexeddb`.
- Keep type exports (`ToolCallPair`, `RenderedTurn`) intact when renaming the function so `AgentChatMessage.tsx` and tests still compile.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit current behavior (read-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. Confirm PaneControlTab slot model (`PaneControlTab.tsx:55-67`, `168-196`, `201-292`).
2. Confirm TasksPane wire-up (`TasksPane.tsx:146-160`, `190-216`, `219-272`, `348-369`).
3. Confirm AgentChat surface to remove and the type exports to preserve (`AgentChat.tsx:24-31` types, `184-188` positioning, `611-613` route gate, `617-691` collapsed UI, `692-976` expanded UI, `979-983` portal).
4. Grep for callers that import `ToolCallPair` or `RenderedTurn` from `./AgentChat` (`AgentChatMessage.tsx`, any tests). Inventory what must keep compiling after the rename.
5. Confirm `<AgentChat />` is mounted only at `src/app/App.tsx:72`.

### Step 2: Extend `PaneControlTab` with a split-button slot (`src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add `splitButton?: { left: PaneControlAction; right: PaneControlAction }` to the `actions` prop (line 60-63).
2. Add `'split'` to the `ButtonType` union (line 17).
3. In `getButtonsToShow()` (168-196), append `'split'` to right-side non-bottom desktop branches **only when `!isLocked && splitButton`**. Do not include it in the locked array — this satisfies "hide when locked" structurally.
4. Add `case 'split':` to `renderButton()`:
   ```tsx
   <div key="split" className="flex flex-row gap-0.5">
     <TooltipButton tooltip={splitButton.left.tooltip} ...>
       <Button ... onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(splitButton.left.onClick)} aria-label={splitButton.left.ariaLabel}>
         {splitButton.left.content}
       </Button>
     </TooltipButton>
     <TooltipButton tooltip={splitButton.right.tooltip} ...>
       <Button ... onPointerUp={handleButtonClick(splitButton.right.onClick)} aria-label={splitButton.right.ariaLabel}>
         {splitButton.right.content}
       </Button>
     </TooltipButton>
   </div>
   ```
   Each half uses a roughly half-width button so the pair fits the same vertical cell as a single regular button. Match `iconSize` token for the contents.

### Step 3: Add a ref-backed actions registry to the agent-chat bridge (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small-Medium

This step is the most subtle — it must avoid re-render churn (correctness-2) and stale captures (callers).

1. Add two contexts side-by-side with the existing clip bridge:
   ```ts
   type AgentChatActionsHandlers = {
     toggleRecording: () => void;
     focusComposer: () => void;
   };
   type AgentChatActionsValue = AgentChatActionsHandlers & {
     isRecording: boolean;
     isProcessing: boolean;
   };
   type AgentChatActionsRegistry = {
     registerHandlers: (handlers: AgentChatActionsHandlers) => void;
     publishState: (state: { isRecording: boolean; isProcessing: boolean }) => void;
     unregister: () => void;
   };
   const AgentChatActionsContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(null);
   const AgentChatActionsRegistryContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsRegistry | null>(null);
   ```
2. Inside `AgentChatProvider`:
   - Hold a `handlersRef = useRef<AgentChatActionsHandlers | null>(null)`. **Imperative methods read through this ref** so callers always invoke the latest registered handler — no stale closures, even if a `requestAnimationFrame` callback fires after a route change unmounts the panel (the ref will be `null` and the call becomes a safe no-op).
   - Hold reactive `useState<{isRecording: boolean; isProcessing: boolean} | null>(null)` for `isRecording` / `isProcessing`. This is what triggers consumer re-renders when voice state changes.
   - Memoize `registry`: `registerHandlers`, `publishState`, `unregister` — all stable via `useCallback([], ...)`.
   - Memoize the consumer-facing `actions`:
     ```ts
     const actions = useMemo<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(() => {
       if (!reactiveState) return null;
       return {
         toggleRecording: () => handlersRef.current?.toggleRecording(),
         focusComposer: () => handlersRef.current?.focusComposer(),
         isRecording: reactiveState.isRecording,
         isProcessing: reactiveState.isProcessing,
       };
     }, [reactiveState]);
     ```
   - Wrap children in both `AgentChatActionsContext.Provider value={actions}` and `AgentChatActionsRegistryContext.Provider value={registry}`.
3. Export hooks:
   - `useAgentChatActions(): AgentChatActionsValue | null` — returns `null` until `AgentChatPanel` mounts and publishes state. **Document inline** that this is the one optional bridge surface (per CLAUDE.md settled decision).
   - `useAgentChatActionsRegistry(): AgentChatActionsRegistry` — `requireContextValue(...)` so it throws if a panel tries to register outside the provider tree.

### Step 4: Strip floating UI; rename to `AgentChatPanel`; preserve type exports (`src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`, `index.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium

The file path stays `AgentChat.tsx`; only the function and the default export rename to `AgentChatPanel`. This keeps `import { ToolCallPair } from './AgentChat'` working in `AgentChatMessage.tsx` and any tests (all_locations-2).

1. **Rename**: `export function AgentChat()` → `export function AgentChatPanel()`. Update `index.ts` to `export { AgentChatPanel } from './AgentChat'`. Add a deprecated re-export only if any caller still references `AgentChat` (audit with grep; today only `App.tsx` does, and Step 6 removes it).
2. **Preserve type exports**: keep `export type ToolCallPair`, `export type RenderedTurn` exactly as they are at lines 24–31. Run `tsc --noEmit` after the rename to confirm `AgentChatMessage.tsx` and tests still compile.
3. **Strip floating-window scaffolding:**
   - Delete `isOpen` state (line 171), the closed-state recording overlay (617-648), the collapsed-pill block with bubble + standalone mic + unseen indicator (649-691), and the `if (!isToolPage) return null` gate (147-148, 611-613). Parent now controls mounting.
   - Delete `positionStyle` and the `usePanesStore` selector that drives it (160-188).
   - Replace the outer `fixed z-50 ...` with `flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-hidden` so the panel sizes to its parent.
   - Replace `createPortal(content, document.body)` (979-983) with a direct return.
4. **Drop closed-state-only state**: remove `lastSeenAssistantTurnCount`, `assistantTurnCount`, `hasUnseenMessages`, `showPendingIndicator`, `showUnseenIndicator`, and the effects that maintain them (596-605). Confirm via grep that nothing else reads them. Keep the inline `Thinking…` row (794-799) and the inline recording UI (874-897) which already cover all feedback the popup used to show.
5. **Auto-create-session gate** (382-399): the current condition `(!isOpen && !voice.isRecording && !voice.isProcessing)` becomes redundant — the panel is always mounted when present. Simplify to: only auto-create when `hasTimeline && sessionOptions.length === 0 && !sessions.isLoading && !createSession.isPending`. Keep the `hasAutoCreatedSessionRef` guard.
6. **Stabilize voice handlers and register exactly once per mount** (correctness-2):
   ```tsx
   const actionsRegistry = useAgentChatActionsRegistry();
   const voiceRef = useRef(voice);
   voiceRef.current = voice;
   const composerRef = inputRef; // already exists

   const stableHandlers = useMemo<AgentChatActionsHandlers>(() => ({
     toggleRecording: () => {
       const v = voiceRef.current;
       if (v.isRecording) v.stopRecording();
       else if (!v.isProcessing) v.startRecording();
     },
     focusComposer: () => composerRef.current?.focus(),
   }), []); // genuinely stable; reads via refs

   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.registerHandlers(stableHandlers);
     return () => actionsRegistry.unregister();
   }, [actionsRegistry, stableHandlers]); // both stable → effect runs once

   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.publishState({
       isRecording: voice.isRecording,
       isProcessing: voice.isProcessing,
     });
   }, [actionsRegistry, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing]);
   ```
   This avoids the `voice.startRecording` identity churn the gate flagged: the registered handlers never change identity, only the small reactive state object does, and only when `isRecording`/`isProcessing` actually flip.
7. Keep the global Cmd+Shift+R shortcut (426-445) verbatim — it already calls `voice.startRecording`/`voice.stopRecording` on the live closure inside the panel.

### Step 5: Restructure TasksPane (`src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Header rename** (line 192): `<h2>Tasks</h2>` → `<h2>Action</h2>`. Update `display.paneTooltip` (line 150) `"View all tasks"` → `"Open Action pane"` and `thirdButton.ariaLabel` (line 154) wording. State/file/icon names unchanged per settled decision.
2. **Tighten the filter row** (lines 219-272): collapse the two-row layout into one flex row of three equal-width buttons:
   ```tsx
   <div className="bg-zinc-800 rounded-lg p-1">
     <div className="flex gap-1">
       {(['Processing','Succeeded','Failed'] as FilterGroup[]).map((filter) => (
         <Button key={filter} variant={selectedFilter === filter ? 'default' : 'ghost'}
           size="sm" onClick={() => handleFilterChange(filter)}
           className={cn('flex-1 text-xs flex items-center justify-center', ...)}>
           <span>{filter}</span>
           <StatusIndicator count={countFor(filter)} type={filter}
             isSelected={selectedFilter === filter}
             onClick={filter !== 'Processing' ? () => handleStatusIndicatorClick(filter) : undefined} />
         </Button>
       ))}
     </div>
   </div>
   ```
   Add a small local `countFor()` helper mapping `Processing` → `cancellableTaskCount`, `Succeeded` → `displayStatusCounts?.recentSuccesses ?? 0`, `Failed` → `displayStatusCounts?.recentFailures ?? 0`. Drop to `text-[11px]` if the trio overflows the default `tasksPaneWidth`.
3. **Vertical split**: wrap the existing top section (header → filter row → `PaginationControls` → warning → `TaskList`) in `flex-1 min-h-0 flex flex-col overflow-hidden`. Below it, render:
   ```tsx
   {isToolRoute && (
     <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 border-t border-zinc-800">
       <AgentChatPanel />
     </div>
   )}
   ```
   `isToolRoute` is a local computed value:
   ```ts
   const { pathname } = useLocation();
   const isToolRoute = pathname.startsWith('/tools') || pathname === '/shots' || pathname === '/art';
   ```
4. **Wire the split button** on `<PaneControlTab>` (lines 146-160):
   ```tsx
   const actions = useAgentChatActions(); // null until AgentChatPanel registers
   ...
   actions={{
     thirdButton: { /* unchanged active-task indicator */ },
     splitButton: actions && isToolRoute
       ? {
           left: {
             onClick: () => {
               openPane();
               // focusComposer is ref-backed; safe even if panel unmounts before frame fires.
               requestAnimationFrame(() => actions.focusComposer());
             },
             ariaLabel: 'Open message composer',
             tooltip: 'Open message',
             content: <MessageSquareText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
           },
           right: {
             onClick: () => actions.toggleRecording(),
             ariaLabel: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start voice recording',
             tooltip: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop' : 'Voice (⌘⇧R)',
             content: actions.isRecording
               ? <Square className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
               : <Mic className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
           },
         }
       : undefined,
   }}
   ```
5. **First-paint behavior** (correctness-1): `useAgentChatActions()` returns `null` for the first render on a tool route, then non-null after `AgentChatPanel`'s registration effect runs. The split button therefore appears one paint after mount. This is acceptable: the user typically interacts with the pane many frames after mount, and the alternative (synchronous registration during render) would violate React rules. Tests in Step 7 assert via `await waitFor(...)` that the split button appears after registration.

### Step 6: Remove the standalone AgentChat mount (`src/app/App.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Delete `import { AgentChat } from '@/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat'` (line 24) and the `<AgentChat />` JSX (line 72).
2. Grep for any other `<AgentChat` JSX usage and confirm none remains. If any code still imports the old name, fix it now (Step 4 audit should have already inventoried these).

### Step 7: Tests for the new integration points (`AgentChat.test.tsx`, `AppProviders.test.tsx`, `PaneControlTab.test.tsx`, `TasksPane.test.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium

These tests directly cover the gate's correctness and all_locations concerns.

1. **`AgentChatContext.tsx` (or a new `AgentChatContext.test.tsx`)** — bridge unit tests:
   - `useAgentChatActions()` returns `null` before any registration.
   - After `registerHandlers + publishState`, returns a non-null object with the published `isRecording`/`isProcessing`.
   - Imperative methods are stable across re-renders (assert reference equality of `actions.toggleRecording` after a state update on something else).
   - Imperative methods always invoke the **most recently registered** handlers (test stale-closure resistance: register handler A, register handler B, call `actions.toggleRecording()`, assert B ran).
   - After `unregister`, returns `null`.
2. **`AgentChat.test.tsx`** — drop tests covering the closed pill / `isOpen` toggle / floating positioning. Add:
   - Renders inline (no portal to body) with `h-full w-full`.
   - Mounting registers stable handlers exactly once (spy on `registerHandlers`, render with several state-changing props, assert call count = 1).
   - `publishState` is called with current `voice.isRecording`/`voice.isProcessing` and updates when those change.
   - `Cmd+Shift+R` still toggles recording.
3. **`PaneControlTab.test.tsx`** (new or extend existing if present) — slot tests:
   - With `splitButton` undefined and `isLocked: false`, no split rendered.
   - With `splitButton` provided and `isLocked: false`, both halves render with their respective `aria-label`s and clicks invoke their handlers.
   - With `splitButton` provided and `isLocked: true`, no split rendered (locked array excludes 'split').
4. **`TasksPane.test.tsx`** (new) — integration of the registration handshake (all_locations-1):
   - On a tool route, after rendering `<TasksPane>` inside `<AgentChatProvider>`, `await waitFor(() => screen.getByLabelText('Open message composer'))` confirms the split button appears once `AgentChatPanel` registers actions.
   - Locking the pane removes the split button.
   - On a non-tool route, the chat panel is absent and the split button never appears.
   - Filter row renders a single row with all three buttons (`getAllByRole('button', { name: /Processing|Succeeded|Failed/ })`).
5. **`AppProviders.test.tsx`** — extend the bridge contract test to assert the new actions context is wired (default value `null`, registry non-null inside the provider tree, throwing `useAgentChatActionsRegistry` outside the tree).
6. **Run order**:
   ```bash
   npx vitest run src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext \
                  src/shared/components/PaneControlTab \
                  src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane \
                  src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat \
                  src/app/providers/AppProviders.test.tsx
   npx tsc --noEmit
   npx eslint src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx \
              src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx \
              src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane \
              src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat \
              src/app/App.tsx
   npx vitest run
   ```

### Step 8: Manual UI smoke (info-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. `npm run dev`. On `/tools/video-editor`:
   - Lock pane → bottom half shows the chat thread, tasks above; chat scrolls independently; split buttons disappear from the tab handle.
   - Unlock pane → split message + mic buttons appear below the lock; left half opens the pane and focuses the composer; right half toggles recording (icon flips between `Mic` and `Square`); Cmd+Shift+R still toggles recording.
   - Cancel All on tasks still works; pagination unchanged.
2. Switch to `/` (non-tool) → pane shows tasks only; split buttons absent.
3. Lock both Editor and Generations → 50/50 viewport split still applies; `TasksPane` z-index still correct relative to `MediaLightbox` on desktop and mobile.

## Execution Order
1. Step 2 (PaneControlTab split slot) — additive, no consumer impact yet.
2. Step 3 (ref-backed actions registry) — additive surface.
3. Step 4 (AgentChatPanel refactor + type-export preservation + stable handler registration).
4. Step 5 (TasksPane wiring + filter row + split button) — depends on 2, 3, 4.
5. Step 6 (App.tsx cleanup) — only after Step 5 to avoid an unmounted period.
6. Step 7 (tests).
7. Step 8 (manual UI smoke).

## Validation Order
1. Type-check after each code step (`npx tsc --noEmit`) — catches the type-export regression early if Step 4 drops `ToolCallPair`/`RenderedTurn`.
2. Targeted vitest specs for touched files (especially `AgentChatContext` and `TasksPane` registration handshake).
3. Lint on touched files.
4. Full vitest suite.
5. Manual UI smoke on `/tools/video-editor` and `/`.

  "recommendation": "ITERATE",
  "rationale": "The plan is improving \u2014 the previous iteration's correctness/registry concerns (re-render churn, type-export break, stale closures, registration handshake) are genuinely resolved by the ref-backed registry design. But the rev-2 rewrite introduced or unmasked five concrete plan defects, three of which are real bugs the executor should not be left to discover. (1) **issue_hints / FLAG-008 (session auto-create)** is the most consequential: the current `AgentChat` only auto-creates a session when `!isOpen` is false (i.e., when chat is opened or voice is active). Rev 2 removes that guard while now mounting the panel on every tool-route visit, so simply navigating to `/tools/video-editor` would create a `timeline_agent_sessions` row even if the user never opens the pane. The brief's \"Out of scope\" list explicitly says \"Don't change... agent behavior.\" The plan must keep the gating semantically \u2014 e.g., only auto-create when the pane is open (`useTasksPaneState`'s `isOpen` or `isLocked`) **or** voice is active \u2014 without depending on the deleted `isOpen` local state. (2) **correctness-2 / FLAG-006 (attachment lightbox portal)** is a real visual bug: the current code's `createPortal(content, document.body)` is what makes the embedded `MediaLightbox` render full-screen. Removing the shell portal without explicitly wrapping the lightbox in its own `createPortal(<MediaLightbox/>, document.body)` will trap it inside the bottom-half of the pane. The plan's assumption text says \"attachment lightbox continues to use `createPortal`\" but Step 4 doesn't add the wrapper. (3) **scope / FLAG-007 (mobile split-button)** is an internal inconsistency: the plan's assumption claims mobile/tablet parity but Step 2 only adds `'split'` to the desktop right-side branches in `getButtonsToShow()`. Either commit to mobile parity (extend the `!useDesktopBehavior && !isBottom` branch) or update the assumption to say desktop-only. (4) **correctness-1 (type export)** is a trivial compilation issue \u2014 `AgentChatActionsHandlers` is referenced in `AgentChat.tsx` but only declared in `AgentChatContext.tsx`; export it. (5) **FLAG-003 (naming)** is a settled gate decision and stays disputed. The Iteration Pressure Analysis shows no `addressed_then_reopened` patterns and no fuzzy group with reopened flags \u2265 2 \u2014 these are first-iteration concerns, not stuck disagreements, so TIEBREAKER doesn't apply. Score is essentially flat (13.5 \u2192 14.0) but that masks real progress: 6 flags resolved offset by 5 new but addressable ones. Send back for one more pass focused tightly on these five items.",
  "signals_assessment": "Iteration 2, weighted score 13.5 \u2192 14.0 (essentially flat), plan delta 60.5% (major rewrite), 6 prior flags resolved (registry re-render churn, type exports, stale closures, registration handshake, parent/child timing, naming-as-disputed), 5 new significant concerns raised by v2 critique. No flags reopened, no fuzzy group with addressed-then-reopened pattern that would justify TIEBREAKER. Recurring critiques (criteria 10/13 require human UI verification) are inherent to UI tasks, not loop churn. Preflight clean.   "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 2,
    "idea": "# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename \u2192 \"Action Pane\")\n\n## Goal\n\nEliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to \"Action Pane\" since it's now broader than tasks.\n\n## Concrete requirements\n\n### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)\n\nCurrently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add \u2014 **below the lock** \u2014 a single split button (two halves side-by-side):\n\n- **Left half:** \"open message\" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.\n- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.\n\nWhen the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).\n\n### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split\n\n- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).\n- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.\n- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.\n\n### 3. Tighten the task list header\n\nCurrently arranged as: \"processing\" on the top row, then \"succeeded\" + \"failed\" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.\n\n### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box\n\nThe popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.\n\n### 5. Rename consideration\n\nThe pane today is called the \"task pane\" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to \"action pane\" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself \u2014 just relocate it.\n- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.\n- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.\n- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.\n\n## Notes for the planner\n\n- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.\n- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.\n- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.\n- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.\n- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing \u2014 don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.\n- Be careful about scope \u2014 the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.",

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

        Idea:
# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename → "Action Pane")

## Goal

Eliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to "Action Pane" since it's now broader than tasks.

## Concrete requirements

### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)

Currently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add — **below the lock** — a single split button (two halves side-by-side):

- **Left half:** "open message" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.
- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.

When the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).

### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split

- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).
- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.
- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.

### 3. Tighten the task list header

Currently arranged as: "processing" on the top row, then "succeeded" + "failed" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.

### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box

The popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.

### 5. Rename consideration

The pane today is called the "task pane" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to "action pane" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.

## Out of scope

- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself — just relocate it.
- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.
- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.
- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.

## Notes for the planner

- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.
- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.
- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.
- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.
- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing — don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.
- Be careful about scope — the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.

        Current plan (markdown):

# Implementation Plan: Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Action Pane (rev 3)

## Overview

Fold the floating bottom-right `AgentChat` popup into the right-side `TasksPane` so the pane becomes a vertical split — task list on top, agent message thread on the bottom. Tighten the task filter row, add a "open-message + mic" split button below the lock on the pane control tab (visible only when not locked), remove the standalone popup, and rename the pane's display label to "Action".

**Settled decisions (carried over; do not re-litigate):**

- **Display-only rename** (gate iter 1). Header/tooltip/aria become `Action`. Code-level names (`TasksPane`, `tasksPaneWidth`, `isTasksPaneLocked`, `paneIcon: 'tasks'`, `⌥D` shortcut hint) stay as-is.
- **Route gate preserved** (gate iter 1). `AgentChatPanel` and the split button stay gated to `/tools|/shots|/art`.
- **`useAgentChatActions()` may return `null`** (gate iter 1). The one optional bridge surface; consumers explicitly hide UI on `null`.
- **Ref-backed actions registry with separate `publishState`** (gate iter 2). Stable handlers; effect runs once per mount; reactive state published on a sibling channel.
- **`AgentChat.tsx` file path stays; type exports preserved** (gate iter 2). Only the function/default export renames to `AgentChatPanel`. `ToolCallPair` and `RenderedTurn` keep their existing export sites.

**Settled this iteration:**

- **Session auto-create must remain gated on user engagement.** The brief's "out of scope: don't change agent behavior" forbids regressing to "create on route load". Replacement gate (mirrors old semantics): `isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing`. `AgentChatPanel` reads pane state from `usePanesStore` and combines with voice state.
- **Attachment lightbox keeps its full-screen overlay** by being explicitly wrapped in `createPortal(..., document.body)` inside `AgentChatPanel`. The shell portal goes away; the lightbox portal is added.
- **Split button is desktop+tablet only** (matches existing `useDesktopBehavior` gate in `PaneControlTab`). On mobile phones the right-side tab already only shows `['third']`; we don't expand that. This drops the rev-2 mobile-parity claim.

**Repo shape (key files):**

- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx` — header line 192, `<PaneControlTab>` 146–160, filter row 219–272, task list 348–369. Also reads `isTasksPaneLocked`, `isTasksPaneOpen` from `panesStore`.
- `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx` — `actions.thirdButton`/`fourthButton`; `getButtonsToShow()` 168–196; `renderButton()` 201–292; `useDesktopBehavior` guard line 107.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx` — `isOpen` (171), `positionStyle` (184–188), auto-create effect (382–399, gated by `(!isOpen && !voice.isRecording && !voice.isProcessing)`), Cmd+Shift+R (426–445), collapsed pill (649–691), recording overlay (617–648), expanded panel (692–976), attachment `MediaLightbox` (967–974), `createPortal(content, document.body)` (979–983), `isToolPage` gate (611). Type exports `ToolCallPair`/`RenderedTurn` at 24–31.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChatMessage.tsx` — `import type { ToolCallPair } from './AgentChat'`.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/index.ts` — re-exports `AgentChat`.
- `src/app/App.tsx:24,72` — mounts `<AgentChat />`.
- `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx` — existing register/unregister bridge.
- `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts` — owns `isTasksPaneLocked`, `isTasksPaneOpen`, `tasksPaneWidth`, `deriveLayout()` 50/50 split. **Untouched.**
- `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useAgentVoice.ts` + `src/shared/hooks/useVoiceRecording` — `startRecording` identity churns when the inline `onTranscription` callback identity changes.

**Constraints:**

- Preserve the recent z-index fix and Editor+Generations 50/50 viewport split.
- Don't regress agent behavior: session auto-create must remain gated on user engagement.
- Don't touch `.claude/`, `.megaplan/`, or `vendor/fake-indexeddb`.
- Type symbols (`AgentChatActionsHandlers`, `AgentChatActionsValue`, `AgentChatActionsRegistry`) are exported from `AgentChatContext.tsx` so cross-module references compile.
- Attachment lightbox stays full-screen via its own `createPortal` once the shell portal is removed.

### Step 3: Add a ref-backed actions registry — and **export the types** (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small-Medium

1. Add and **export** the type surface so consumers in `AgentChat.tsx` can reference them:
   ```ts
   export type AgentChatActionsHandlers = {
     toggleRecording: () => void;
     focusComposer: () => void;
   };
   export type AgentChatActionsValue = AgentChatActionsHandlers & {
     isRecording: boolean;
     isProcessing: boolean;
   };
   export type AgentChatActionsRegistry = {
     registerHandlers: (handlers: AgentChatActionsHandlers) => void;
     publishState: (state: { isRecording: boolean; isProcessing: boolean }) => void;
     unregister: () => void;
   };
   const AgentChatActionsContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(null);
   const AgentChatActionsRegistryContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsRegistry | null>(null);
   ```
2. Inside `AgentChatProvider`:
   - `const handlersRef = useRef<AgentChatActionsHandlers | null>(null);` — imperative methods read through this ref so callers always invoke the latest handler. If the panel unmounts (route change), the ref is `null` and any pending `requestAnimationFrame` callback becomes a safe no-op.
   - `const [reactiveState, setReactiveState] = useState<{ isRecording: boolean; isProcessing: boolean } | null>(null);` — drives consumer re-renders for `isRecording`/`isProcessing`.
   - Stable `registry`:
     ```ts
     const registerHandlers = useCallback((h) => { handlersRef.current = h; }, []);
     const publishState = useCallback((s) => { setReactiveState((prev) =>
       prev && prev.isRecording === s.isRecording && prev.isProcessing === s.isProcessing
         ? prev : s
     ); }, []);
     const unregister = useCallback(() => {
       handlersRef.current = null;
       setReactiveState(null);
     }, []);
     const registry = useMemo(() => ({ registerHandlers, publishState, unregister }),
       [registerHandlers, publishState, unregister]);
     ```
   - Memoize the consumer `actions`:
     ```ts
     const actions = useMemo<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(() => {
       if (!reactiveState) return null;
       return {
         toggleRecording: () => handlersRef.current?.toggleRecording(),
         focusComposer: () => handlersRef.current?.focusComposer(),
         isRecording: reactiveState.isRecording,
         isProcessing: reactiveState.isProcessing,
       };
     }, [reactiveState]);
     ```
   - Wrap children in both `AgentChatActionsContext.Provider value={actions}` and `AgentChatActionsRegistryContext.Provider value={registry}`.
3. Export hooks:
   - `useAgentChatActions(): AgentChatActionsValue | null` — returns `null` until the panel mounts and publishes state. Inline comment explains this is the one optional surface (settled).
   - `useAgentChatActionsRegistry(): AgentChatActionsRegistry` — `requireContextValue(...)`; throws if used outside the provider.

**Scope:** Medium

Confirm via grep that nothing else reads them. Inline `Thinking…` (794-799) and inline recording UI (874-897) cover all feedback the popup used to show.
5. **Preserve session auto-create gating** (issue_hints / FLAG-008 fix):
   - Read pane state inline: `const { isTasksPaneLocked, isTasksPaneOpen } = usePanesStore((s) => ({ isTasksPaneLocked: s.isTasksPaneLocked, isTasksPaneOpen: s.isTasksPaneOpen }), shallow);`.
   - Update the auto-create effect (382-399) to keep the same "user has engaged" early-return as before, just sourced from new signals:
     ```tsx
     useEffect(() => {
       const isPaneEngaged = isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen;
       const isVoiceActive = voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing;
       if (
         hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current
         || sessions.isLoading
         || createSession.isPending
         || sessionOptions.length > 0
         || !hasTimeline
         || (!isPaneEngaged && !isVoiceActive) // ← preserves old (!isOpen && !voice.isRecording && !voice.isProcessing)
       ) {
         return;
       }
       hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current = true;
       createSession.mutate(undefined, {
         onError: () => { hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current = false; },
         onSuccess: (session) => { setActiveSessionId(session.id); },
       });
     }, [createSession, hasTimeline, sessionOptions.length, sessions.isLoading,
         isTasksPaneLocked, isTasksPaneOpen, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing]);
     ```
   - Net effect: visiting a tool route with the pane closed and no voice active does **not** auto-create a session, matching the old `(!isOpen && !voice.isRecording && !voice.isProcessing)` gate. Only locking the pane, calling `openPane()` (e.g. via the message split button), or activating voice triggers session creation.
6. **Portal the attachment lightbox** (correctness-2 / FLAG-006 fix):
   - The shell `createPortal(content, document.body)` (979-983) goes away — return `content` directly.
   - **Wrap the `<MediaLightbox />` block** that currently lives inline (967-974, inside the shell) in its own portal so it stays full-screen instead of being constrained to the bottom half of the pane:
     ```tsx
     {attachmentLightboxMedia && createPortal(
       <MediaLightbox
         media={attachmentLightboxMedia}
         initialVariantId={attachmentLightboxMedia.primary_variant_id ?? undefined}
         onClose={handleCloseAttachmentLightbox}
         features={{ showDownload: true, showTaskDetails: true }}
       />,
       document.body,
     )}
     ```
   - Keep the existing `import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'`.
7. **Stabilize voice handlers and register exactly once per mount** (correctness-2 from iter 1, kept):
   ```tsx
   import type { AgentChatActionsHandlers } from '@/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext';
   const actionsRegistry = useAgentChatActionsRegistry();
   const voiceRef = useRef(voice);
   voiceRef.current = voice;

   const stableHandlers = useMemo<AgentChatActionsHandlers>(() => ({
     toggleRecording: () => {
       const v = voiceRef.current;
       if (v.isRecording) v.stopRecording();
       else if (!v.isProcessing) v.startRecording();
     },
     focusComposer: () => inputRef.current?.focus(),
   }), []); // genuinely stable; deps empty; reads via refs

   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.registerHandlers(stableHandlers);
     return () => actionsRegistry.unregister();
   }, [actionsRegistry, stableHandlers]); // both stable → effect runs once per mount

   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.publishState({
       isRecording: voice.isRecording,
       isProcessing: voice.isProcessing,
     });
   }, [actionsRegistry, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing]);
   ```
8. Keep the global Cmd+Shift+R shortcut (426-445) verbatim.

       {(['Processing','Succeeded','Failed'] as FilterGroup[]).map((filter) => (
         <Button key={filter} variant={selectedFilter === filter ? 'default' : 'ghost'}

(3) Step 3 explicitly `export`s the `AgentChatActionsHandlers`/`Value`/`Registry` types so `AgentChat.tsx` can reference them \u2014 fixes correctness-1 (TS compile). (4) Step 2 + assumptions now consistently say desktop+tablet only for the split button (matching the existing `useDesktopBehavior` guard); the rev-2 mobile-parity claim is dropped \u2014 resolves scope/FLAG-007. Step 7 gains explicit tests for the auto-create gate and the lightbox portal, and Step 8 adds manual checks for both regressions. Naming-scope (FLAG-003) stays disputed/settled.",
  "flags_addressed": [
    {
      "id": "issue_hints",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Step 4 part 5 preserves the auto-create gate semantically: AgentChatPanel reads `isTasksPaneLocked` and `isTasksPaneOpen` from usePanesStore and combines with voice.isRecording/isProcessing to mirror the old `(!isOpen && !voice.isRecording && !voice.isProcessing)` early-return. Visiting a tool route with the pane closed and voice idle no longer creates a session. Step 7.2 adds explicit tests; Step 8 adds a manual check on first paint."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-008",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same fix as issue_hints \u2014 auto-create now gated on `isPaneEngaged || isVoiceActive`, sourced from panesStore. Effect deps include the new gating signals. No behavior regression vs. the old isOpen-based gate."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-2",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Step 4 part 6 explicitly wraps the attachment MediaLightbox JSX in createPortal(<MediaLightbox/>, document.body) so it remains a full-screen overlay after the shell portal is removed. The existing react-dom createPortal import is reused. Step 7.2 adds an assertion that the lightbox is portaled to document.body, and Step 8 adds a manual check."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-006",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same fix as correctness-2 \u2014 attachment lightbox now portals independently. The plan and assumptions are consistent: only the chat shell stops portaling, the lightbox is explicitly re-portaled."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness-1",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Step 3 part 1 explicitly `export`s AgentChatActionsHandlers, AgentChatActionsValue, and AgentChatActionsRegistry types from AgentChatContext.tsx. Step 4 part 7 adds the cross-module type import: `import type { AgentChatActionsHandlers } from '@/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext'`. tsc --noEmit in the validation order catches any remaining cross-module type issues."
    },
    {
      "id": "scope",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Resolved the rev-2 internal inconsistency by committing to desktop+tablet only (matching existing useDesktopBehavior guard). Step 2 part 3 explicitly says NOT to modify the mobile-phone branch; assumptions updated to match. Step 7.3 adds a mobile-phone test that asserts the split is omitted on phones (intentional)."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-007",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same fix as scope \u2014 Step 2 plus assumptions now consistently scope the split to useDesktopBehavior (desktop + tablet). Brief is desktop-focused; mobile-phone right-side panes already only show one button, and the brief did not request a phone-specific UI affordance for this work."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-003",
      "resolution": "rejected",
      "reason": "Marked disputed/settled by the gate (settled-decision: naming-scope, iter 1). The gate's rationale explicitly says do not re-litigate. Plan reaffirms the settled decision in Overview rather than expanding scope."
    }
  ],
  "questions": [
    "Left-half message button: confirm intent is openPane() + focusComposer() against the existing session (no new session). Plan keeps existing AgentChatPanel auto-create behavior in charge of session creation \u2014 but auto-create is now gated on engagement, so clicking the message split button (which calls openPane()\u2192sets isTasksPaneOpen=true) is what unlocks session creation. That matches the brief's intent (clicking message = engaged with chat) but please confirm this is the desired user flow."
  ],

  "rationale": "The plan is genuinely converging \u2014 score dropped 13.5 \u2192 14.0 \u2192 9.5, deltas decreased 60% \u2192 38%, 11 flags resolved with zero reopens, and the new fixes (auto-create gate preservation, lightbox portal, type exports, mobile-scope clarification) are all sound. But the v3 critique caught one subtle implementation defect: the auto-create gate engagement signal mismatches the message-button caller. Five of the six open flags (issue_hints, callers, FLAG-008, correctness, all_locations) are restatements of the same concrete bug \u2014 `useSlidingPane.openPane()` only sets local `hoverOpen`, not the store-backed `setIsTasksPaneOpen(true)` that Step 4's auto-create gate (`isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing`) reads. So as written, clicking the new \"Open message\" split button would open the pane and focus the composer but **not** satisfy the engagement gate, leaving the user staring at an empty chat with no session created. This is a real user-facing bug, not a paranoid edge case. The fix is small \u2014 one of three options the next revision should pick: (a) the message-button click handler should call the panes-store setter directly (e.g. `setIsTasksPaneOpen(true)` or the appropriate action) in addition to `openPane()`, so the engagement signal is set; (b) extend `useTasksPaneSlidingPane.onOpenChange` so programmatic opens write `setIsTasksPaneOpen(true)` (not just `false`); or (c) re-source the gate from the local `useSlidingPane` open state via a small bridge so it tracks whatever `openPane()` actually sets. Step 7's auto-create test must also exercise the actual click path from `TasksPane` (currently it only tests direct store transitions, which is what the all_locations flag points out \u2014 the test would pass while the user flow fails). Given the iteration discipline (no reopens, score dropping, deltas shrinking, single-issue convergence) and the concrete one-spot fix, ITERATE is correct. TIEBREAKER doesn't apply: FG-001/FG-002 have 1 flag each at 3 iterations with 0 reopens, well below the \u22652-flags-across-\u22652-iterations threshold, and this isn't an architectural tension \u2014 it's a missed implementation detail the next pass can pin down. ESCALATE would be wrong because the loop is making clear progress toward a focused, addressable fix.",

  "signals": {
    "iteration": 3,
    "idea": "# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename \u2192 \"Action Pane\")\n\n## Goal\n\nEliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to \"Action Pane\" since it's now broader than tasks.\n\n## Concrete requirements\n\n### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)\n\nCurrently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add \u2014 **below the lock** \u2014 a single split button (two halves side-by-side):\n\n- **Left half:** \"open message\" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.\n- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.\n\nWhen the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).\n\n### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split\n\n- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).\n- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.\n- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.\n\n### 3. Tighten the task list header\n\nCurrently arranged as: \"processing\" on the top row, then \"succeeded\" + \"failed\" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.\n\n### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box\n\nThe popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.\n\n### 5. Rename consideration\n\nThe pane today is called the \"task pane\" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to \"action pane\" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself \u2014 just relocate it.\n- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.\n- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.\n- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.\n\n## Notes for the planner\n\n- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.\n- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.\n- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.\n- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.\n- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing \u2014 don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.\n- Be careful about scope \u2014 the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.",

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Calling both is intentional belt-and-braces:\n               // setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic flips the store-backed engagement signal so AgentChatPanel's\n               // auto-create gate (`isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing`)\n               // becomes true; openPane() drives the local sliding animation in case the programmaticOpen\n               // round-trip lags a frame. Without the explicit setter, useSlidingPane.openPane() only sets\n               // local hoverOpen and the gate would never fire on this click path.\n               setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true);\n               openPane();\n               requestAnimationFrame(() => actions.focusComposer());\n             },\n             ariaLabel: 'Open message composer',\n             tooltip: 'Open message',\n             content: <MessageSquareText className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />,\n           },\n           right: {\n             onClick: () => actions.toggleRecording(),\n             ariaLabel: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start voice recording',\n             tooltip: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop' : 'Voice (⌘⇧R)',\n             content: actions.isRecording\n               ? <Square className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n               : <Mic className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />,\n           },\n         }\n       : undefined,\n   }}\n   ```\n5. **First-paint behavior**: `useAgentChatActions()` returns `null` for the first render on a tool route, then non-null after `AgentChatPanel`'s registration effect runs. Toggling each of `isTasksPaneLocked`, `isTasksPaneOpen`, `voice.isRecording`, `voice.isProcessing` to `true` from store/voice mocks → `mutate` is called once on the transition.\n   - Attachment lightbox is portaled to `document.body` (assert it's rendered as a child of `document.body`, not a descendant of the panel root).\n3. **`PaneControlTab.test.tsx`** — slot tests:\n   - Desktop: `splitButton` undefined + `!isLocked` → no split rendered.\n   - Desktop: `splitButton` provided + `!isLocked` → both halves render with their `aria-label`s; clicks invoke their handlers.\n   - Desktop: `splitButton` provided + `isLocked` → no split rendered.\n   - Mobile phone (`useDesktopBehavior=false`, side `right`): `splitButton` provided → still only `['third']` rendered (intentional).\n4. **`TasksPane.test.tsx`** — integration tests covering the **actual click path** (all_locations fix):\n   - On a tool route, render `<TasksPane>` inside `<AgentChatProvider>` and the panes-store provider, with `hasTimeline=true` and no session. `await waitFor(() => screen.getByLabelText('Open message composer'))` confirms the split appears once `AgentChatPanel` registers.\n   - **Click-path auto-create test (the gate-flagged scenario)**: with the pane initially closed and voice idle, click the `'Open message composer'` button. > AGENT

Revision 4 delivered. Single-line bug fix for the engagement-signal mismatch the gate flagged five times:

- **Step 5.4 click handler** now calls `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` (the existing local-name binding for `panesStore.setIsTasksPaneOpen`) before `openPane()` and `requestAnimationFrame(focusComposer)`. That explicit store write is what makes `isTasksPaneOpen` flip to `true` on the click, satisfying `AgentChatPanel`'s auto-create gate (`isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing`). `useSlidingPane.openPane()` no longer needs to be load-bearing for the gate.
- **No contract change** to `useSlidingPane` or `useTasksPaneSlidingPane` — gate option (a) was chosen as the smallest scoped fix.
- **Step 7.4 adds the click-path test** the `all_locations` flag said was missing: simulates a real button click and asserts `isTasksPaneOpen` becomes true → `focusComposer` is invoked → `createSession.mutate` is called exactly once on the AgentChatPanel re-render. Mic-button variant covered too.
- **Step 8** adds an explicit manual smoke check that clicking the message split button creates a session in the network tab (and that plain route load with pane closed does not).
- **FLAG-003** stays disputed/settled per the gate's iter-1 ruling.

# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename → "Action Pane")

## Goal

Eliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to "Action Pane" since it's now broader than tasks.

## Concrete requirements

### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)

Currently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add — **below the lock** — a single split button (two halves side-by-side):

- **Left half:** "open message" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.
- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.

When the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).

### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split

- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).
- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.
- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.

### 3. Tighten the task list header

Currently arranged as: "processing" on the top row, then "succeeded" + "failed" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.

### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box

The popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.

### 5. Rename consideration

The pane today is called the "task pane" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to "action pane" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.

## Out of scope

- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself — just relocate it.
- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.
- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.
- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.

## Notes for the planner

- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.
- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.
- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.
- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.
- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing — don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.
- Be careful about scope — the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.

# Implementation Plan: Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Action Pane (rev 4)

## Overview

Fold the floating bottom-right `AgentChat` popup into the right-side `TasksPane` so the pane becomes a vertical split — task list on top, agent message thread on the bottom. Tighten the task filter row, add a "open-message + mic" split button below the lock on the pane control tab (visible only when not locked), remove the standalone popup, and rename the pane's display label to "Action".

**Settled decisions (carried over; do not re-litigate):**

- **Display-only rename** (gate iter 1).
- **Route gate preserved** (gate iter 1).
- **`useAgentChatActions()` may return `null`** (gate iter 1).
- **Ref-backed actions registry with separate `publishState`** (gate iter 2).
- **`AgentChat.tsx` file path stays; type exports preserved** (gate iter 2).
- **Lightbox portal**: chat shell stops portaling; the attachment `<MediaLightbox />` is wrapped in its own `createPortal(..., document.body)` so it remains full-screen (gate iter 3).
- **Split button is desktop+tablet only**, matching the existing `useDesktopBehavior` guard (gate iter 3).
- **Auto-create gate is sourced from `usePanesStore` engagement (locked/open) plus voice activity** — never from a deleted local `isOpen` (gate iter 3).

**Settled this iteration (FLAG-008 / issue_hints / callers / correctness / all_locations resolution):**

- The message split button must explicitly write the **store-backed** engagement signal so the auto-create gate fires. Concretely, the click handler calls `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` (the existing local name in `TasksPane.tsx` for `panesStore.setIsTasksPaneOpen`) **in addition to** `openPane()`. `useSlidingPane.openPane()` only sets local `hoverOpen` and does not route through `programmaticOpen`, so the store flag would otherwise stay false and `AgentChatPanel`'s auto-create gate would not satisfy. The new explicit setter call closes that gap; the existing `useTasksPaneSlidingPane.onOpenChange` keeps responsibility for clearing the flag on close. No other change to the sliding-pane contract.
- Tests must exercise the **actual click path** end-to-end (TasksPane click → store update → AgentChatPanel auto-create), not just direct store transitions.

**Repo shape (key files):**

- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/TasksPane.tsx` — header line 192, `<PaneControlTab>` 146–160, filter row 219–272, task list 348–369. Already reads `isTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic` and `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic` from `panesStore` (lines 44–45) — we'll reuse this setter.
- `src/features/tasks/components/TasksPane/hooks/useTasksPaneSlidingPane.ts` — passes `programmaticOpen: isTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic`, clears it via `onOpenChange(false)`. **Untouched** — no contract change.
- `src/shared/hooks/useSlidingPane` — `openPane()` only sets local hover state; we don't modify it.
- `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx` — `actions.thirdButton`/`fourthButton`; `getButtonsToShow()` 168–196; `useDesktopBehavior` line 107.
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx` — see rev 3 inventory; preserves type exports `ToolCallPair`/`RenderedTurn` (24–31).
- `src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChatMessage.tsx`, `index.ts` — type-import surface preserved.
- `src/app/App.tsx:24,72` — mounts `<AgentChat />`; will be removed.
- `src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx` — extended with exported actions registry types/hooks.
- `src/shared/state/panesStore.ts` — owns `isTasksPaneOpen`/`setIsTasksPaneOpen`/`isTasksPaneLocked`. **Untouched.**

**Constraints:**

- Don't regress agent behavior: session auto-create stays gated on user engagement. Plain route load with the pane closed and voice idle must not create a session.
- Don't change `useSlidingPane` or `useTasksPaneSlidingPane` contracts; the click handler does the explicit store write.
- Preserve recent z-index fix and Editor+Generations 50/50 viewport split.
- Don't touch `.claude/`, `.megaplan/`, or `vendor/fake-indexeddb`.

### Step 3: Add a ref-backed actions registry — and **export the types** (`src/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small-Medium
1. Add and **export** the type surface so consumers in `AgentChat.tsx` can reference them via `import type`:
   ```ts
   export type AgentChatActionsHandlers = {
     toggleRecording: () => void;
     focusComposer: () => void;
   };
   export type AgentChatActionsValue = AgentChatActionsHandlers & {
     isRecording: boolean;
     isProcessing: boolean;
   };
   export type AgentChatActionsRegistry = {
     registerHandlers: (handlers: AgentChatActionsHandlers) => void;
     publishState: (state: { isRecording: boolean; isProcessing: boolean }) => void;
     unregister: () => void;
   };
   const AgentChatActionsContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(null);
   const AgentChatActionsRegistryContext = createContext<AgentChatActionsRegistry | null>(null);
   ```
2. Inside `AgentChatProvider`:
   - `const handlersRef = useRef<AgentChatActionsHandlers | null>(null);` — imperative methods read through this ref so callers always invoke the latest handler (stale-closure-safe).
   - `const [reactiveState, setReactiveState] = useState<{ isRecording: boolean; isProcessing: boolean } | null>(null);` — drives consumer re-renders.
   - Stable `registry` (memoized via `useCallback([], …)`):
     ```ts
     const registerHandlers = useCallback((h) => { handlersRef.current = h; }, []);
     const publishState = useCallback((s) => { setReactiveState((prev) =>
       prev && prev.isRecording === s.isRecording && prev.isProcessing === s.isProcessing
         ? prev : s
     ); }, []);
     const unregister = useCallback(() => {
       handlersRef.current = null;
       setReactiveState(null);
     }, []);
     const registry = useMemo(() => ({ registerHandlers, publishState, unregister }),
       [registerHandlers, publishState, unregister]);
     ```
   - Memoized consumer `actions`:
     ```ts
     const actions = useMemo<AgentChatActionsValue | null>(() => {
       if (!reactiveState) return null;
       return {
         toggleRecording: () => handlersRef.current?.toggleRecording(),
         focusComposer: () => handlersRef.current?.focusComposer(),
         isRecording: reactiveState.isRecording,
         isProcessing: reactiveState.isProcessing,
       };
     }, [reactiveState]);
     ```
   - Wrap children in both `AgentChatActionsContext.Provider value={actions}` and `AgentChatActionsRegistryContext.Provider value={registry}`.
3. Export hooks:
   - `useAgentChatActions(): AgentChatActionsValue | null` — `null` until panel mounts and publishes state. Inline comment: optional surface (settled).
   - `useAgentChatActionsRegistry(): AgentChatActionsRegistry` — `requireContextValue(...)`, throws outside the provider.

### Step 4: Refactor AgentChat → AgentChatPanel; preserve type exports; preserve session-gating; portal the lightbox (`src/tools/video-editor/components/AgentChat/AgentChat.tsx`, `index.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Rename**: `export function AgentChat()` → `export function AgentChatPanel()`. Update `index.ts`: `export { AgentChatPanel } from './AgentChat'`. File path unchanged.
2. **Preserve type exports** at lines 24–31: `export type ToolCallPair`, `export type RenderedTurn`. `tsc --noEmit` after this step.
3. **Strip closed-state UI:**
   - Delete `isOpen` (171), closed-state recording overlay (617-648), collapsed-pill block (649-691, including standalone mic + chat bubble + unseen indicator), and `isToolPage` gate (147-148, 611-613).
   - Delete the old `positionStyle` selector that read `tasksPaneWidth`/generations-pane height (160-188); replace with a smaller selector for the auto-create gate (next bullet).
   - Replace the outer `fixed z-50 ...` shell with `flex h-full w-full flex-col overflow-hidden`.
4. **Drop closed-only state**: remove `lastSeenAssistantTurnCount`, `assistantTurnCount`, `hasUnseenMessages`, `showPendingIndicator`, `showUnseenIndicator`, and the maintaining effects (596-605). Confirm via grep no other consumers. Inline `Thinking…` (794-799) and inline recording UI (874-897) stay.
5. **Preserve session auto-create gating** (sources from store):
   ```tsx
   const { isTasksPaneLocked, isTasksPaneOpen } = usePanesStore((s) => ({
     isTasksPaneLocked: s.isTasksPaneLocked,
     isTasksPaneOpen: s.isTasksPaneOpen,
   }), shallow);

   useEffect(() => {
     const isPaneEngaged = isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen;
     const isVoiceActive = voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing;
     if (
       hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current
       || sessions.isLoading
       || createSession.isPending
       || sessionOptions.length > 0
       || !hasTimeline
       || (!isPaneEngaged && !isVoiceActive)
     ) {
       return;
     }
     hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current = true;
     createSession.mutate(undefined, {
       onError: () => { hasAutoCreatedSessionRef.current = false; },
       onSuccess: (session) => { setActiveSessionId(session.id); },
     });
   }, [createSession, hasTimeline, sessionOptions.length, sessions.isLoading,
       isTasksPaneLocked, isTasksPaneOpen, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing]);
   ```
   Plain route load with pane closed and voice idle does **not** create a session — the gate only flips when the user locks the pane, clicks the new message split button (which writes `isTasksPaneOpen=true` via the store; see Step 5.4), or activates voice.
6. **Portal the attachment lightbox** (preserves full-screen overlay):
   - Remove the shell `createPortal(content, document.body)` (979-983) — return `content` directly.
   - Wrap the existing `<MediaLightbox />` JSX (967-974) in its own portal:
     ```tsx
     {attachmentLightboxMedia && createPortal(
       <MediaLightbox
         media={attachmentLightboxMedia}
         initialVariantId={attachmentLightboxMedia.primary_variant_id ?? undefined}
         onClose={handleCloseAttachmentLightbox}
         features={{ showDownload: true, showTaskDetails: true }}
       />,
       document.body,
     )}
     ```
   - Keep the existing `import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'`.
7. **Stabilize voice handlers, register exactly once per mount:**
   ```tsx
   import type { AgentChatActionsHandlers } from '@/shared/contexts/AgentChatContext';
   const actionsRegistry = useAgentChatActionsRegistry();
   const voiceRef = useRef(voice);
   voiceRef.current = voice;

   const stableHandlers = useMemo<AgentChatActionsHandlers>(() => ({
     toggleRecording: () => {
       const v = voiceRef.current;
       if (v.isRecording) v.stopRecording();
       else if (!v.isProcessing) v.startRecording();
     },
     focusComposer: () => inputRef.current?.focus(),
   }), []); // genuinely stable; deps empty; reads via refs

   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.registerHandlers(stableHandlers);
     return () => actionsRegistry.unregister();
   }, [actionsRegistry, stableHandlers]);

   useEffect(() => {
     actionsRegistry.publishState({
       isRecording: voice.isRecording,
       isProcessing: voice.isProcessing,
     });
   }, [actionsRegistry, voice.isRecording, voice.isProcessing]);
   ```
8. Keep the global Cmd+Shift+R shortcut (426-445) verbatim.
