4. **Wire the split button** on `<PaneControlTab>` (146-160) — **the message-button click handler explicitly writes the store-backed engagement signal so the auto-create gate fires** (issue_hints / callers / FLAG-008 / correctness fix):
   ```tsx
   const actions = useAgentChatActions(); // null until AgentChatPanel registers
   // setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic is already in scope at line 45 (mapped from panesStore.setIsTasksPaneOpen)
   ...
   actions={{
     thirdButton: { /* unchanged active-task indicator */ },
     splitButton: actions && isToolRoute
       ? {
           left: {
             onClick: () => {
               // Two writes: store flag (drives auto-create gate + programmaticOpen) and openPane()
               // (matches the existing third-button pattern). Calling both is intentional belt-and-braces:
               // setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic flips the store-backed engagement signal so AgentChatPanel's
               // auto-create gate (`isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing`)
               // becomes true; openPane() drives the local sliding animation in case the programmaticOpen
               // round-trip lags a frame. Without the explicit setter, useSlidingPane.openPane() only sets
               // local hoverOpen and the gate would never fire on this click path.
               setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true);
               openPane();
               requestAnimationFrame(() => actions.focusComposer());
             },
             ariaLabel: 'Open message composer',
             tooltip: 'Open message',
             content: <MessageSquareText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
           },
           right: {
             onClick: () => actions.toggleRecording(),
             ariaLabel: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop recording' : 'Start voice recording',
             tooltip: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop' : 'Voice (⌘⇧R)',
             content: actions.isRecording
               ? <Square className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
               : <Mic className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />,
           },
         }
       : undefined,
   }}
   ```

6. **No change to `useSlidingPane` or `useTasksPaneSlidingPane`** — the click handler does the explicit store write. The existing `onOpenChange(false)` close-clear path in `useTasksPaneSlidingPane` is sufficient: locking, voice activity, and message-button click all set the store flag explicitly via different paths; closing the pane clears it via `onOpenChange`.

### Step 8: Manual UI smoke (info-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. `npm run dev`. On `/tools/video-editor`:
   - **Refresh on `/tools/video-editor` with the pane closed** → no `timeline_agent_sessions` insert in the network tab. (FLAG-008 regression check.)
   - **Click the message split button** (left half, below the lock) → pane slides open, composer is focused, **a session is created** (network shows the insert). (issue_hints / callers / FLAG-008 / correctness verification: the click path now writes the store flag, so the gate fires.)
   - **Click the mic split button** (right half) → recording starts; on stop, a session is created if none existed.
   - **Lock the pane** → bottom half shows the chat thread; tasks above; chat scrolls independently; split buttons disappear from the tab handle; session is created if none existed.
   - Open an attachment from a chat message → the `MediaLightbox` covers the full viewport, not constrained to the pane bottom. (FLAG-006 verification.)
   - Cmd+Shift+R → toggles recording; if no session existed, voice activity creates one.
   - Cancel All on tasks still works; pagination unchanged.
2. Switch to `/` (non-tool) → pane shows tasks only; split buttons absent.
3. Lock both Editor and Generations → 50/50 viewport split still applies; `TasksPane` z-index correct relative to `MediaLightbox` on desktop and mobile.
4. (Mobile phone simulation) → tab handle still shows just the tasks count; split is omitted by design.

  "hash": "sha256:584a113337477e04f764698d77245fceb509e3e6ce5d6cf08919d9b5d9d38c76",
  "changes_summary": "Single concrete fix targeting the bug the gate flagged five times: the message split-button click handler in TasksPane now explicitly calls `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` (which maps to `panesStore.setIsTasksPaneOpen`) before `openPane()`. That writes the store-backed engagement signal that AgentChatPanel's auto-create gate reads, closing the gap where `useSlidingPane.openPane()` only set local `hoverOpen` and never triggered session creation on the primary click path. No change to `useSlidingPane` or `useTasksPaneSlidingPane` contracts (gate option a). Step 7.4 gains a specific click-path integration test that simulates the actual user click and asserts (a) `isTasksPaneOpen` becomes true, (b) `focusComposer` is called, (c) `createSession.mutate` is called exactly once on the re-render. This is the test the gate said was missing. Step 8 adds an explicit manual smoke check that clicking the message split button creates a session. Naming-scope (FLAG-003) stays disputed/settled.",
  "flags_addressed": [
    {
      "id": "issue_hints",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Step 5.4 click handler now writes the store-backed engagement signal explicitly: `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` runs before `openPane()` and `focusComposer()`. This makes `panesStore.isTasksPaneOpen` true on the click, satisfying the auto-create gate `isTasksPaneLocked || isTasksPaneOpen || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing`. Inline comment in the plan documents why both calls are present (belt-and-braces: store write drives the gate, openPane() drives the slide animation)."
    },
    {
      "id": "callers",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "The new click handler is the actual caller for the message split button. It now invokes both `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` and `openPane()`, so the store-backed engagement signal that AgentChatPanel's auto-create effect reads is reliably set. `useSlidingPane.openPane()`'s local-only behavior is no longer load-bearing for the gate."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-008",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Same fix as issue_hints/callers \u2014 the click path now writes the store flag explicitly. AgentChatPanel mounts on tool routes but only auto-creates when (a) pane is locked, (b) `isTasksPaneOpen=true` (now reliably set by message-button click, by lock toggle via existing `useTasksPaneSlidingPane.onOpenChange(false)` cleanup contract, and by other consumers), or (c) voice is active."
    },
    {
      "id": "correctness",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Auto-create gate is no longer under-specified against the actual pane opener. The plan calls out that `useSlidingPane.openPane()` only sets local hoverOpen and that the click handler must therefore write the store flag itself. No contract change to useSlidingPane or useTasksPaneSlidingPane. Step 1.3 adds an explicit audit step to verify this contract before implementation."
    },
    {
      "id": "all_locations",
      "resolution": "addressed",
      "reason": "Step 7.4 now includes a click-path integration test that exercises TasksPane click \u2192 store update \u2192 AgentChatPanel auto-create end-to-end. Asserts (a) isTasksPaneOpen flips to true after the click, (b) focusComposer is invoked, (c) createSession.mutate is called once on the re-render. The mic-button variant is also tested. The original direct-store-transition tests stay (Step 7.2) for unit coverage."
    },
    {
      "id": "FLAG-003",
      "resolution": "rejected",
      "reason": "Disputed/settled across iterations 1\u20133 (settled-decision: naming-scope). The brief explicitly says 'be careful about scope \u2014 the user asked for these specific changes only'. A code-level rename across state, file paths, and the panes store is the kind of out-of-scope refactor the brief forbids. Plan reaffirms the settled decision in Overview rather than expanding scope."
    }
  ],
  "questions": [
    "Confirm the chosen fix flavor: the message-button click handler explicitly calls `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` (writing `panesStore.isTasksPaneOpen`) in addition to `openPane()`. Alternative options the gate considered were (b) extending `useTasksPaneSlidingPane.onOpenChange` to write the store on programmatic-open and (c) re-sourcing the gate from local sliding-pane state. Plan picks (a) because it is the smallest scoped change and does not touch the sliding-pane contract. If you prefer (b) or (c), say so and we'll adjust."
  ],

      "id": "issue_hints",
      "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: The new message-click flow may not preserve the pane's normal close behavior. In the current repo, once `programmaticOpen` is true, `useSlidingPane.setOpen(false)` returns early and `closePane()` only clears local `hoverOpen`; `useTasksPaneSlidingPane.onOpenChange(false)` cannot fire while programmatic open is still holding `isOpen=true`.   "recommendation": "ITERATE",
  "rationale": "The pattern across iterations 2\u20134 is a textbook over-determined-constraint loop, not a plan-quality problem. Each iteration's fix is technically correct against the prior critique but uncovers another wrinkle in the same subsystem: iter 2 said \"session auto-create regressed because `isOpen` was deleted\"; iter 3 fixed it with a `panesStore.isTasksPaneOpen` gate but introduced \"the message button doesn't write that flag\"; iter 4 added an explicit `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)` write and now the critique points out that once `programmaticOpen` is true, `useSlidingPane.setOpen(false)` short-circuits and `useTasksPaneSlidingPane.onOpenChange` can never clear the flag \u2014 the pane stays open with no normal close path. The plan writer is doing real work each round (score 9.5, 11 prior flags resolved) but every fix touches the same fragile join between three independent state systems (local `hoverOpen`, store `programmaticOpen`, derived `isOpen`) that were not designed to express \"user has engaged with chat.\" FG-001 and FG-002 each have 1 flag across 4 iterations; FG-004 and FG-005 are at 3 iterations; FG-010 has 9 flags across 4 iterations. This meets the TIEBREAKER spirit (multiple groups with sustained iteration activity in one subsystem, score stuck at 9.5\u201310.5 with growing plan deltas) even though no individual group hit the strict \u22652-flags-across-\u22652-iterations threshold. The constraint set is over-determined: (1) \"don't change agent behavior\" forbids letting auto-create fire on plain mount, (2) the floating popup's local `isOpen` flag is gone, (3) the plan keeps committing to \"don't modify `useSlidingPane`/`useTasksPaneSlidingPane` contracts,\" and (4) the only remaining engagement signal \u2014 `panesStore.isTasksPaneOpen` \u2014 is owned by a sliding-pane state machine whose close semantics depend on the very flag the plan now writes from a third path. Something has to give, and the plan writer keeps gravitating toward the same insufficient point fix because they're not authorized to relax any of those four constraints. This is a human call about which constraint to relax. ESCALATE would also be defensible (the loop is stuck) but TIEBREAKER is the cleaner framing because the resolution is a discrete design choice with three concrete options, not \"step in and intervene.\" Cited evidence: FG-001 (iter 1\u21924), FG-002 (iter 1\u21924), FG-004 (iter 2\u21924), FG-005 (iter 2\u21924), FG-014 (iter 3\u21924); score regression 9.5 \u2192 10.5; plan-delta inflation 38% \u2192 51%.   "signals": {
    "iteration": 4,
    "idea": "# Integrate Timeline Agent Chat Into the Task Pane (rename \u2192 \"Action Pane\")\n\n## Goal\n\nEliminate the floating bottom-right timeline-agent chat box. Fold it into the existing task pane so the pane becomes a top/bottom split: tasks on top, the agent's message thread on the bottom. Probably rename the pane to \"Action Pane\" since it's now broader than tasks.\n\n## Concrete requirements\n\n### 1. Pane control tab (the side handle that opens the pane)\n\nCurrently shows the active task indicator and a lock button. Add \u2014 **below the lock** \u2014 a single split button (two halves side-by-side):\n\n- **Left half:** \"open message\" button. Opens the pane and focuses the chat / message composer in the bottom half.\n- **Right half:** microphone button. Triggers the same handler the existing standalone microphone button uses today.\n\nWhen the pane is **locked open**, the two new buttons on the pane control tab must hide (the chat is already visible in the pane, so they're redundant).\n\n### 2. Pane layout becomes a vertical split\n\n- **Top half:** existing task list (processing / succeeded / failed).\n- **Bottom half:** the timeline-agent message thread that today lives in a popup. Now lives here. Must scroll the same way the message thread scrolls today.\n- The whole pane should remain scrollable / navigable between the two halves the way the pane works today.\n\n### 3. Tighten the task list header\n\nCurrently arranged as: \"processing\" on the top row, then \"succeeded\" + \"failed\" on a second row. Change to a single row: **processing | succeeded | failed**, side-by-side. Make the buttons smaller if needed to fit. The point is to give the actual task list more vertical space.\n\n### 4. Remove the standalone bottom-right timeline-agent chat box\n\nThe popup chat box anchored to the bottom-right corner goes away entirely. Everything it does is now part of the action pane's bottom half.\n\n### 5. Rename consideration\n\nThe pane today is called the \"task pane\" (or pane control tab). Since it now hosts both tasks and the agent chat, consider renaming to \"action pane\" or similar. Pick a name that makes sense in code and UI.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Don't redesign the chat / message thread itself \u2014 just relocate it.\n- Don't redesign the lock or active-task indicator.\n- Don't change task creation, task status semantics, or agent behavior.\n- Don't add new keyboard shortcuts beyond what already exists for the popup mic / message composer.\n\n## Notes for the planner\n\n- This spans pane-control UI, the existing task pane component, the existing timeline-agent chat popup, and the standalone mic button. All of those probably need to move or change.\n- A `.claude/` directory in the repo root is local-only Claude config and should be ignored.\n- A vendored `vendor/fake-indexeddb` directory exists for tests; do not touch it.\n- The pane control tab logic recently changed (z-index fix, 50/50 viewport split when both Editor and Generations locked). Any new changes should preserve that behavior.\n- The motivating UX principle from CLAUDE.md: required-provider hooks must throw when context is missing \u2014 don't paper over missing providers with no-op fallbacks.\n- Be careful about scope \u2014 the user asked for these specific changes only. Don't refactor surrounding code beyond what the changes require.",

        Requirements:
        - Before addressing individual flags, check: does any flag suggest the plan is targeting the wrong code or the wrong root cause? If so, consider whether the plan needs a new approach rather than adjustments. Explain your reasoning.
        - Update the plan to address the significant issues.
        - Keep the plan readable and executable.
        - Return flags_addressed with the exact flag IDs you addressed.
        - Include `changes_summary` as a short plain-English summary of what changed in the revision. If there were no concrete flags, say that explicitly (for example: `No critique flags were raised; refined wording and kept the plan aligned for execution.`).
        - Preserve or improve success criteria quality. Each criterion must have a `priority` of `must`, `should`, or `info`. Promote or demote priorities if critique feedback reveals a criterion was over- or under-weighted.
        - Verify that the plan remains aligned with the user's original intent, not just internal plan quality.
        - Remove unjustified scope growth. If critique raised scope creep, narrow the plan back to the original idea unless the broader work is strictly required.
        - Maintain the structural template: H1 title, ## Overview, phase sections with numbered step sections, ## Execution Order or ## Validation Order.
        - CRITICAL: Your entire revised plan markdown (all sections) must be output as the `plan` field in the structured output. The prose response must not contain the plan text.
        - CRITICAL: Return only the structured JSON object for the schema fields `plan`, `changes_summary`, `flags_addressed`, `assumptions`, `success_criteria`, and `questions`. Do not add commentary before or after the JSON object.

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Below it:\n   ```tsx\n   {isToolRoute && (\n     <div className=\"flex-1 min-h-0 border-t border-zinc-800\">\n       <AgentChatPanel />\n     </div>\n   )}\n   ```\n   ```ts\n   const { pathname } = useLocation();\n   const isToolRoute = pathname.startsWith('/tools') || pathname === '/shots' || pathname === '/art';\n   ```\n4. **Wire the split button** on `<PaneControlTab>` (146-160) — **the message-button click handler calls `actions.markEngaged()` (no panes-store write)**:\n   ```tsx\n   const actions = useAgentChatActions(); // null until AgentChatPanel registers\n   ...\n   actions={{\n     thirdButton: { /* unchanged active-task indicator */ },\n     splitButton: actions && isToolRoute\n       ? {\n           left: {\n             onClick: () => {\n               // Three calls, all local-or-bridge — no panes-store write:\n               // - markEngaged() flips AgentChatPanel's local userEngaged flag, which is the new\n               //   engagement signal in the auto-create gate. 'Stop recording' : 'Start voice recording',\n             tooltip: actions.isRecording ? 'Stop' : 'Voice (⌘⇧R)',\n             content: actions.isRecording\n               ? <Square className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n               : <Mic className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />,\n           },\n         }\n       : undefined,\n   }}\n   ```\n5. **First-paint behavior**: `useAgentChatActions()` returns `null` for the first render on a tool route, then non-null after `AgentChatPanel` registers. The existing close paths (hover-leave clears local hoverOpen; lock toggle clears `isTasksPaneLocked`; backdrop click closes on mobile) all continue to work.\n\n### Step 6: Remove the standalone AgentChat mount (`src/app/App.tsx`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. Add:\n   - Renders inline (no portal of the chat shell to body) with `h-full w-full`.\n   - Mounting registers stable handlers exactly **once** (call count = 1 across multiple state-changing renders).\n   - `publishState` is called with current `voice.isRecording`/`voice.isProcessing` and updates when those change.\n   - Cmd+Shift+R still toggles recording.\n   - **Auto-create gate** (direct unit, no UI):\n     - With no session, `hasTimeline=true`, pane unlocked, `userEngaged=false`, voice idle → `createSession.mutate` is **not** called.\n     - Setting `isTasksPaneLocked=true` (via store mock) → `mutate` called once.\n     - Calling the registered `markEngaged()` handler → `userEngaged` flips, `mutate` called once (only if not already called).\n     - Setting `voice.isRecording=true` → `mutate` called once.\n     - Setting `voice.isProcessing=true` → `mutate` called once.\n   - `userEngaged` does NOT auto-clear (one-shot); `mutate` is still called only once total (`hasAutoCreatedSessionRef` guard).\n   - Attachment lightbox is a child of `document.body`, not a descendant of the panel root.\n3. **`PaneControlTab.test.tsx`** — slot tests (unchanged from rev 4):\n   - Desktop: `splitButton` undefined + `!isLocked` → no split.\n   - Desktop: provided + `!isLocked` → both halves render and click.\n   - Desktop: provided + `isLocked` → no split.\n   - Mobile phone: provided → still only `['third']`.\n4. **`TasksPane.test.tsx`** — integration tests covering the actual click path:\n   - On a tool route, render `<TasksPane>` inside `<AgentChatProvider>`. `await waitFor(() => screen.getByLabelText('Open message composer'))` — split appears once panel registers.\n   - **Click-path auto-create test (replaces rev-4 store-write check)**: with the pane unlocked, no session, voice idle, click `'Open message composer'`. Assert (a) `actions.markEngaged()` was invoked (spy on the registered handler), (b) `openPane()` ran (e.g. transformClass changes / pane slides in via local hover state), (c) `actions.focusComposer()` invoked on the next frame, (d) `createSession.mutate` is called exactly once on the resulting AgentChatPanel re-render. **No assertion that `panesStore.isTasksPaneOpen` flipped to true** — that flag is intentionally not used by the new gate.\n   - Mic-button variant: clicking the mic toggles voice → `voice.isRecording=true` → gate true → `createSession.mutate` called once.\n   - **Close-path test (rev-5 cleanup-risk fix, all_locations / FLAG-009)**: after the message-button click opens the pane and creates a session, simulate a hover-leave / backdrop close. > AGENT

Revision 5 delivered. Replaces the rev-4 store-write approach (which created a close-path bug) with **Option C from the gate**: a dedicated local engagement flag.

Key changes:

- **Step 4**: Auto-create gate now reads `isTasksPaneLocked || userEngaged || voice.isRecording || voice.isProcessing` where `userEngaged` is a `useState` flag local to `AgentChatPanel`. **No read of `panesStore.isTasksPaneOpen`** — that conflation is what caused the cleanup loop.
- **Step 3**: `AgentChatActionsHandlers` gains a `markEngaged()` method, exposed via the existing ref-backed registry.
- **Step 5**: Message-button click handler now calls `actions.markEngaged()` instead of `setIsTasksPaneOpenProgrammatic(true)`. The pane uses its existing local `hoverOpen` mechanism via `openPane()`, so the existing close paths (hover-leave, backdrop, lock toggle) all keep working unchanged. **No contract change** to `useSlidingPane`, `useTasksPaneSlidingPane`, or `panesStore`.
- **Step 7.4**: Adds an explicit close-path test that verifies after a message-button open, hover-leave / backdrop close returns the pane to closed state, and that no panes-store programmaticOpen flag was set during the click flow (so there's no leftover state).
- **Step 8**: Manual smoke now explicitly verifies the close-path: hover away after a click-open returns the pane to closed state.

The fundamental change: "is the pane open" and "has the user engaged with chat" are now independent signals, decoupled at the source. The four-iteration loop of conflation-driven bugs ends here.
